be the event defined as
A_{k}:=\left\{\sup_{i\leq k}\mathrm{d}{(y_{i},y_{0})}\leq{r}_{1}\right\}\cap\left\{\sup_{i\leq k}{\left\|{\tilde{z}}_{i}\right\|}\leq{r}_{1}\right\}
. Then

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{k}\right\}}\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(\tilde{y}_{k+1},y_{k+1})}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{k-1}\right\}}\left({(1+\frac{1}{K})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\beta}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{2}+8\delta L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\xi}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+2^{27}K{({L_{R}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{4}+L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{2})}{(\delta^{4}L_{\beta}^{4}+\delta^{2}L_{\xi}^{4})}\right)

where
\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\cdot}\right]
denotes expectation conditioned on
\xi_{0}...\xi_{k-1}
.

Proof of Lemma
16
.

For the rest of this proof, consider a fixed
k
. We will always condition on the event
A_{k}
, but to reduce notation we will not state this explicitly throughout the proof. Whenever we use
\Gamma_{y_{0}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}
, we refer to parallel transport along the geodesic
\Exp_{y_{0}}{(t\tilde{z}_{k})}
.

By our assumptions in (
C.19
), and by definition of the event
A_{k}
, we guarantee that

\displaystyle 1.\ \mathrm{d}{(y_{k},y_{0})}\leq{r}_{1}\leq{\mathcal{C}}_{r}

\displaystyle 2.\ {\left\|{\tilde{z}}_{k}\right\|}\leq{r}_{1}\leq{\mathcal{C}}_{r}

\displaystyle 3.\ {\left\|\delta\beta(y_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(y_{k})\right\|}\leq\delta L_{\beta}+\sqrt{\delta}L_{\xi}\leq{\mathcal{C}}_{r}

\displaystyle 4.\ {\left\|\delta\beta(y_{0})+\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{y_{0}}{(\xi_{k}{(\tilde{y}_{k})})}\right\|}\leq\delta L_{\beta}+\sqrt{\delta}L_{\xi}\leq{\mathcal{C}}_{r}

(C.20)

where the last two lines use (
C.19
).

Now consider a fixed
k
. Let us apply Lemma
78
with
x:=y_{0}
,
u:=\tilde{z}_{k}
,
v:=\delta\beta(y_{0})+\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{\tilde{y}_{k}}^{y_{0}}{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))}
, and
x^{\prime}:=\Exp_{x}(u)=\tilde{y}_{k}
(note that the conditions on
{\left\|u\right\|}\leq{\mathcal{C}}_{r}
and
{\left\|v\right\|}\leq{\mathcal{C}}_{r}
are verified in (
C.20
)), which guarantees that there exists a tensor
G
such that

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{x}{(u+v)},\Exp_{x^{\prime}}{(\Gamma_{x}^{x^{\prime}}G(v))})}\leq

\displaystyle 2^{12}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{({\left\|u\right\|}+{\left\|v\right\|})}^{2}+L_{R}{({\left\|u\right\|}+{\left\|v\right\|})})}{\left\|v\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{13}{(L_{R}^{\prime}r_{1}^{2}+L_{R}r_{1})}{(\delta^{2}L_{\beta}^{2}+\delta L_{\xi}^{2})}

Notice from our definition of
\tilde{y}_{k}
that the
\tilde{y}_{k+1}=\Exp_{x}(u,v)
, so that

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(\tilde{y}_{k+1},\Exp_{x^{\prime}}{(\Gamma_{x}^{x^{\prime}}G(v))})}\leq 2^{13}{(L_{R}^{\prime}r_{1}^{2}+L_{R}r_{1})}{(\delta^{2}L_{\beta}^{2}+\delta L_{\xi}^{2})}

(C.21)

We will establish shortly that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(y_{k+1},\Exp_{x^{\prime}}{(\Gamma_{x}^{x^{\prime}}G(v))})}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1+\frac{1}{K})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\beta}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{2}+8\delta L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\xi}^{2}

(C.22)

where
\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\cdot}\right]
denotes expectation with respect to the randomness in
\xi_{k}
, conditioned on
\xi_{0}...\xi_{k-1}
. Combining (
C.21
) and (
C.22
), and explicitly writing the
{\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{k}\right\}}
term,

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{k}\right\}}\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(\tilde{y}_{k+1},y_{k+1})}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{k}\right\}}{({(1+\frac{1}{2K})}\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{x^{\prime}}{(\Gamma_{x}^{x^{\prime}}G(v))},{y}_{k+1})}^{2}}\right]+K\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{x^{\prime}}{(\Gamma_{x}^{x^{\prime}}G(v))},\tilde{y}_{k+1})}^{2}}\right])}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{k}\right\}}\left({(1+\frac{1}{K})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\beta}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{2}+8\delta L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\xi}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+2^{27}K{({L_{R}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{4}+L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{2})}{(\delta^{4}L_{\beta}^{4}+\delta^{2}L_{\xi}^{4})}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{k-1}\right\}}\left({(1+\frac{1}{K})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\beta}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{2}+8\delta L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\xi}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+2^{27}K{({L_{R}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{4}+L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{2})}{(\delta^{4}L_{\beta}^{4}+\delta^{2}L_{\xi}^{4})}\right)

where the last inequallity is because
{\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{k}\right\}}\leq{\mathbbm{1}\left\{A_{k-1}\right\}}
for all
k
, by definition of
A_{k}
. This proves the lemma.

For the rest of this proof, we will prove the bound in (
C.22
). Recall that we earlier defined
v:=\delta\beta(y_{0})+\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{\tilde{y}_{k}}^{y_{0}}\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k})
. From the definition of
G(v)
(see Lemma
78
), we verify that
G(v)
is linear in
v
. In particular,

\displaystyle G(v)=\delta G{(\beta(y_{0}))}+\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{\tilde{y}_{k}}^{y_{0}}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))}

Let us also define

\displaystyle\tilde{v}:=\Gamma_{y_{0}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}G(v)\qquad\qquad\hat{v}:=\delta\beta(y_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(y_{k})

so that
y_{k+1}=\Exp_{y_{k}}(\hat{v})
and
\Exp_{x^{\prime}}{(\Gamma_{x}^{x^{\prime}}G(v))}=\Exp_{\tilde{y}_{k}}(\tilde{v})
. Bounding
\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(y_{k+1},\Exp_{x^{\prime}}{(\Gamma_{x}^{x^{\prime}}G(v))})}^{2}}\right]
from (
C.22
) is thus equivalent to bounding
\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{y_{k}}(\hat{v}),\Exp_{\tilde{y}_{k}}(\tilde{v}))}^{2}}\right]
.

Our main tool is Lemma
63
. To this end, we will first verify that
L_{R}{({\left\|\tilde{v}\right\|}^{2}+{\left\|\hat{v}\right\|}^{2})}\leq 1/4
: From Lemma
78
, we can bound
{\left\|G-Id\right\|}\leq\frac{1}{3}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{3}L_{R}r_{1}^{2}\leq 1
, where the last step uses (
C.20
) and the definition of
{\mathcal{C}}_{r}
in (
C.12
). Therefore,
L_{R}{\left\|\tilde{v}\right\|}=L_{R}{\left\|G(v)\right\|}^{2}\leq 4L_{R}{\left\|v\right\|}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{8}
. Similarly, we can bound
L_{R}{\left\|v\right\|}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{8}
.

By Lemma
63
,

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{y_{k}}(\hat{v}),\Exp_{\tilde{y}_{k}}(\tilde{v}))}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1+8L_{R}{({\left\|G(v)\right\|}+{\left\|\hat{v}\right\|})}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+32{\left\|\tilde{v}-\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\hat{v}\right\|}^{2}+2\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\hat{v}-\tilde{v}\bm{}

(C.23)

where
\gamma(t):[0,1]\to M
is any minimizing geodesic from
\tilde{y}_{k}
to
y_{k}
, and
\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}
denotes parallel transport along
\gamma
.

Using our bound on
{\left\|G\right\|}\leq 1+{\left\|G-Id\right\|}\leq 2
above, the first term of (
C.23
) can be bounded as

\displaystyle{(1+8L_{R}{({\left\|G(v)\right\|}+{\left\|\hat{v}\right\|})}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1+16L_{R}{({\left\|v\right\|}+{\left\|\hat{v}\right\|})}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1+32L_{R}{(\delta^{2}L_{\beta}^{2}+\delta L_{\xi}^{2})})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1+\frac{1}{8K})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}

where the last inequality uses the bound on
K\delta
from (
C.19
).

We can bound the second term of (
C.23
) as

\displaystyle{\left\|\tilde{v}-\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\hat{v}\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|\delta\Gamma_{y_{0}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}G{(\beta(y_{0}))}+\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))}-\delta\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\beta(y_{k})-\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\delta^{2}{\left\|G{(\beta(y_{0}))}-\beta(y_{0})\right\|}^{2}+8\delta^{2}{\left\|\Gamma_{y_{0}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\beta(y_{0})-\beta(\tilde{y}_{k})\right\|}^{2}+8\delta^{2}{\left\|\beta(\tilde{y}_{k})-\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\beta(y_{k})\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+8\delta{\left\|G(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k})-\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k})\right\|}^{2}+8{\left\|\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k})-\xi_{k}(y_{k})\right\|}^{2}

By (
C.20
), Assumption
2
, and our bound on
{\left\|G-I\right\|}
,
{\left\|G{(\beta(y_{0}))}-\beta(y_{0})\right\|}\leq L_{R}r_{1}^{2}\cdot L_{\beta}
. Similarly,
{\left\|G(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k})-\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k})\right\|}\leq L_{R}r_{1}^{2}L_{\xi}
. By Assumption
6
,
{\left\|\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k})-\xi_{k}(y_{k})\right\|}\leq{L_{\xi}^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}
. By Assumption
2
,
{\left\|\Gamma_{y_{0}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\beta(y_{0})-\beta(\tilde{y}_{k})\right\|}\leq{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}r_{1}
and
{\left\|\beta(\tilde{y}_{k})-\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\beta(y_{k})\right\|}\leq{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}
. Put together, we can bound

\displaystyle{\left\|\tilde{v}-\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\hat{v}\right\|}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 8{(\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}+\delta{L_{\xi}^{\prime}}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+8L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}{(\delta^{2}L_{\beta}^{2}+\delta L_{\xi}^{2})}+8\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8K}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+8L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}{(\delta^{2}L_{\beta}^{2}+\delta L_{\xi}^{2})}+8\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{2}

(C.24)

where the last inequality uses the bound on
K\delta
from (
C.19
).

Finally, we bound the third term of (
C.23
):

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\hat{v}-\tilde{v}+{(\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})-\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))})}\bm{}+\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})-\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))}\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(t_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}{\left\|\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\hat{v}-\tilde{v}+{(\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})-\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))})}\right\|}+\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})-\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))}\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2K}\mathrm{d}{(t_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+K{\left\|\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\hat{v}-\tilde{v}+{(\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})-\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))})}\right\|}^{2}+\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})-\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))}\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2K}\mathrm{d}{(t_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\beta}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{2}+\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})-\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))}\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4K}\mathrm{d}{(t_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\beta}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{2}+\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})-\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))}\bm{}

where the second inequality uses very similar steps as (
C.24
), and the last inequality uses the bound on
K\delta
from (
C.19
). The reasoning for our decomposition above is as follows: let
\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{}\right]
denote expectation wrt randomness in
\xi_{k}
conditioned on
\xi_{0}...\xi_{k-1}
. We verify that
\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\bm{\left\langle}\gamma^{\prime}(0),\sqrt{\delta}G{(\xi_{k}(\tilde{y}_{k}))}-\sqrt{\delta}\Gamma_{y_{k}}^{\tilde{y}_{k}}\xi_{k}(y_{k})\bm{}}\right]=0
. In particular, this is because
\xi_{k}
and
G(\xi_{k})
have
0
mean, because
G(v)
is linear in
v
. Plugging everything back into (
C.23
) and taking expectation wrt
\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\cdot}\right]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{k}}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(\Exp_{y_{k}}(\hat{v}),\Exp_{\tilde{y}_{k}}(\tilde{v}))}^{2}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle{(1+\frac{1}{K})}\mathrm{d}{(y_{k},\tilde{y}_{k})}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\beta}^{2}+9K\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}r_{1}^{2}+8\delta L_{R}^{2}r_{1}^{4}L_{\xi}^{2}

where the second inequality is by bounds on
K\delta
from (
C.19
). This concludes the proof of (
C.22
).
∎

Lemma 17
.

Let
c\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
be such that
c\geq 3
. For any
x
satisfying
x\geq 3c\log c
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{x}{\log x}\geq c

Proof.

Let
y:=3c\log c
. We will first verify that
\frac{y}{\log(y)}\geq c\log c
:

\displaystyle c\log(y)=c{(\log c+\log\log c+\log 3)}\leq 3c\log c=y

where we use the fact that
\log\log c\leq\log c
for all
c
, and
\log 3\leq\log c
as
3\leq c
by assumption. Dividing both sides by
\log(y)
gives the desired conclusion.

Next, we note that
\frac{x}{\log(x)}
is monotonically increasing for
x\geq e
. Again by assumption that
c\geq 3
, we verify that
y=3c\log c\geq 3c\geq e
. Therefore, for any
x\geq y
,
\frac{x}{\log x}\geq\frac{y}{\log y}\geq c
. This concludes our proof
∎

C.3
Orthonormal Frame Computations

Lemma 18
.

Given
x\in M
,
u,v\in T_{x}M
, vector field
\xi:M\to TM
. Let
E=\left\{E_{1}...E_{d}\right\}
be an arbitrary orthonormal basis of
T_{x}M
. Let
\mathbf{u},\mathbf{v}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
be vectors of indices of
u
and
v
wrt
E_{i}
.

Let
\xi(x)
be a vector field that satisfies
6
. Let
H(z):T_{x}M\to T_{x}M
be defined as

\displaystyle H(z)=\Gamma_{\Exp_{x}(z)}^{x}{(\xi{(\Exp_{x}(z))})}

where the parallel transport is along the geodesic
\gamma(t)=\Exp_{x}(tz)
. Let us define
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}^{d}
as the vector-valued function, whose
j^{th}
coordinate is given by

\displaystyle{\mathbf{H}}_{j}(\mathbf{z})=\bm{\left\langle}H{(\mathbf{z}\circ E)},E_{j}\bm{}

(recall that
\mathbf{z}\circ E=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathbf{z}_{j}E_{j}
).

Assume that
L_{R}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\leq 1
. Then for all
\mathbf{v},\mathbf{w},\mathbf{z}
,

\displaystyle{\left|\bm{\left\langle}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}_{j}(\mathbf{u}),\mathbf{v}\bm{}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq 4{(L_{\xi}^{\prime}+L_{\xi}L_{R})}{\left\|\mathbf{v}\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left|\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}_{j}(\mathbf{u}),\mathbf{v}\mathbf{w}^{T}\bm{}_{2}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq 208{(L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}+L_{\xi}^{\prime}L_{R}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}+L_{R}^{\prime}L_{\xi}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}+L_{R}L_{\xi})}{\left\|\mathbf{v}\right\|}{\left\|\mathbf{w}\right\|}

\displaystyle\frac{{\left|\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{3}{\mathbf{H}}_{j}(\mathbf{u}),\mathbf{v}\otimes\mathbf{w}\otimes\mathbf{z}\bm{}_{3}\right|}}{{\left\|\mathbf{v}\right\|}{\left\|\mathbf{w}\right\|}{\left\|\mathbf{z}\right\|}}

\displaystyle\leq 8{(L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime\prime}+L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}L_{R}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|})}

\displaystyle\quad+832{(L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}L_{R}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}+L_{\xi}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}+L_{R})})}

\displaystyle\quad+416L_{\xi}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}+L_{R})}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{16}L_{\xi}{({(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|})}+L_{R}^{\prime}+L_{R}L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}^{3})}

Proof of Lemma
18
.

Let
\Lambda(s,t)=\exp_{x}{(t(u+sv))}
.

Let us define a parallel orthonormal frame
E(s,t)=\left\{E_{1}(s,t)...E_{(}s,t)\right\}
as the parallel transport of
E
along the geodesic
\gamma_{s}(t):=\Lambda(s,t)
.

For any
i
, tt follows from our definition that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{H}}_{j}(\mathbf{u}+s\mathbf{v}):=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}\Gamma_{\Exp_{x}(u+sv)}^{x}{(\xi{(\exp_{x}{(u+sv)})})},E_{i}(s,0)\bm{}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(\exp_{x}{(u+sv)})},E_{i}(s,1)\bm{}

where
u=\mathbf{u}\circ E
and
v=\mathbf{v}\circ E
.

Then

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d}{ds}{\mathbf{H}}_{j}{(\mathbf{u}+s\mathbf{v})}\right|_{s=0}=

\displaystyle\left.\frac{d}{ds}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(\exp_{x}{(u+sv)})},E_{i}(s,1)\bm{}\right|_{s=0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left.D_{s}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(\exp_{x}{(u+sv)})},E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}\right|_{s=0,t=1}

We now bound
D_{s}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(\Lambda(s,t))},E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}
.

\displaystyle D_{s}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(\Lambda(s,t))},E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}D_{s}\xi{(\Lambda(s,t))},E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}+\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(\Lambda(s,t))},D_{s}E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}

By definition,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{s}\xi{(\Lambda(s,t))}\right\|}=

\displaystyle{\left\|\bm{\left\langle}\nabla\xi{(\Lambda(s,t))},J(s,t)\bm{}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla\xi\right\|}{\left\|J\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{\xi}^{\prime}{\left\|v\right\|}{(1+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{(L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}e^{L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}}+1)})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{\xi}^{\prime}{\left\|v\right\|}

(using assumption that
{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}L_{R}\leq 1
)

The second term can be simplified using
D_{t}E_{i}(s,t)=0
, so

\displaystyle D_{t}D_{s}E_{i}(s,t)=-R{(J,\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}(s,t)

Integrating the above then gives

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{s}E_{i}(s,t)\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}{\left\|R{(J(s,r),E_{i}(s,r))}\right\|}dr

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L_{R}{\left\|J\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{R}{\left\|v\right\|}

Thus

\displaystyle{\left\|\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(\exp_{x}{(u+sv)})},D_{s}E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}\right\|}\leq 4L_{\xi}L_{R}{\left\|v\right\|}

Summing the bounds on
D_{s}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(\Lambda(s,t))},E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}
and
{\left\|D_{s}E_{i}(s,t)\right\|}
gives

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{s}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(\exp_{x}{(u+sv)})},E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}\right\|}\leq 4{(L_{R}+L_{\xi})}{\left\|v\right\|}

Let us now define a field
\Lambda(r,s,t)=\exp_{x}{(t{(u+rw+sv)})}
. We bound
{\left\|\frac{\partial}{\partial r}\frac{\partial}{\partial s}{\mathbf{H}}{(\mathbf{u}+s\mathbf{v}+r\mathbf{w})}\right\|}
. We can verify that this corresponds to

\displaystyle D_{r}D_{s}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(r,s,t)},E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}D_{r}D_{s}\xi{(r,s,t)},E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{\left\langle}D_{s}\xi{(r,s,t)},D_{r}E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{\left\langle}D_{r}\xi{(r,s,t)},D_{s}E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(r,s,t)},D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{r}D_{s}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(r,s,t)},E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{\xi}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{r}E_{i}(r,s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{\xi}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|D_{s}E_{i}(r,s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{\xi}{\left\|D_{s}D_{s}E_{i}(s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+4L_{\xi}^{\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+4L_{\xi}^{\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+208L_{\xi}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 208{(L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}+L_{\xi}^{\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R}^{\prime}L_{\xi}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R}L_{\xi})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

Where we apply Lemma
21
and Lemma
22
.

Finally, define a field
\Lambda(r,s,t)=\exp_{x}{(t{(u+rw+sv)})}
. We bound
{\left\|\frac{\partial}{\partial q}\frac{\partial}{\partial r}\frac{\partial}{\partial s}{\mathbf{H}}{(\mathbf{u}+s\mathbf{v}+r\mathbf{w}+q\mathbf{z})}\right\|}
. We can verify that this corresponds to

\displaystyle D_{q}D_{r}D_{s}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{}=

\displaystyle D_{q}{(\bm{\left\langle}D_{r}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{})}

\displaystyle\quad+D_{q}{(\bm{\left\langle}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},D_{r}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{})}

\displaystyle\quad+D_{q}{(\bm{\left\langle}D_{r}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},D_{s}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{})}

\displaystyle\quad+D_{q}{(\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{})}

We will bound the norms of each of the terms above separately

\displaystyle D_{q}{(\bm{\left\langle}D_{r}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{})}=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}D_{q}D_{r}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{\left\langle}D_{r}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},D_{q}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}{(\bm{\left\langle}D_{r}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{})}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}D_{r}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+{\left\|D_{r}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}{\left\|D_{q}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime\prime}{\left\|J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{q}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime\prime}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+8L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8{(L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime\prime}+L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle D_{q}{(\bm{\left\langle}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},D_{r}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{})}=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}D_{q}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},D_{r}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{\left\langle}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},D_{q}D_{r}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}{(\bm{\left\langle}D_{s}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},D_{r}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{})}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{r}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{\xi}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{q}D_{r}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+416L_{\xi}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 416{(L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{\xi}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

By symmetry,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}{(\bm{\left\langle}D_{r}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},D_{s}E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{})}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle 416{(L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{\xi}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

Finally,

\displaystyle D_{q}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(r,s,t)},D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}D_{q}\xi{(r,s,t)},D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(r,s,t)},D_{q}D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(r,s,t)},D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}(r,s,t)\bm{}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{\xi}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}(r,s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{\xi}{\left\|D_{q}D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}(r,s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 416L_{\xi}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{16}L_{\xi}{({(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}+L_{R}^{\prime}+L_{R}L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

Where we use Lemma
23
. Thus

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}D_{r}D_{s}\bm{\left\langle}\xi{(q,r,s,t)},E_{i}(q,r,s,t)\bm{}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8{(L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime\prime}+L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+832{(L_{\xi}^{\prime\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{\xi}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+416L_{\xi}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{16}L_{\xi}{({(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}+L_{R}^{\prime}+L_{R}L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

∎

Lemma 19
.

Let
\Lambda(r,s,t):=\exp_{x}{(t{(u+rw+sv)})}
. Assume that
L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{4}
. Then for all
t\leq 1
, and for
s=r=0
,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle 48{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle 96{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

Proof of Lemma
19
.

From the Jacobi Field formula,

\displaystyle D_{r}D_{t}D_{t}J^{s}=

\displaystyle-D_{r}{(R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime})}

Exchanging
D_{r}
and
D_{t}
,

\displaystyle D_{t}D_{r}D_{t}J^{s}=

\displaystyle-D_{r}{(R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime})}-R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}

\displaystyle D_{t}D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}=

\displaystyle-D_{r}{(R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime})}-R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}-D_{t}{(R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s})}

Simplifying the above,

\displaystyle D_{t}^{2}D_{r}J^{s}=

\displaystyle-D_{r}{(R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime})}-R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}-D_{t}{(R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{(D_{r}R)}{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{s},D_{r}\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}

\displaystyle\quad-{(D_{t}R)}{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(D_{t}J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{r},D_{t}\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}

Bounding the norms

\displaystyle{\left\|{(D_{r}R)}{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|R{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq L_{R}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|R{(J^{s},D_{r}\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq L_{R}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|u\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq 4L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq L_{R}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{r}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq 4L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq L_{R}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq 4L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|{(D_{t}R)}{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}^{2}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq 4L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|R{(D_{t}J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq L_{R}{\left\|D_{t}J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq 4L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|R{(J^{r},D_{t}\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle{\left\|R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq L_{R}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{s}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq 4L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

Summing the above,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}^{2}D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}+24{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

Integrating wrt
t
, for
t\leq 1
,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{a}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}+24{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}dadb

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}+24{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}dt

By Gronwall’s Inequality,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle e^{L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}}24{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 48{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

It then also follows that

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle 48L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}+24{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

∎

Lemma 20
.

Let
\Lambda(q,r,s,t):=\exp_{x}{(t{(u+qz+rw+sv)})}
. Assume that
L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}\leq\frac{1}{4}

L_{R}
and
L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}\leq\frac{1}{4}
. Then for all
t\leq 1
, and for
s=r=q=0
,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle 2^{14}{(L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

Proof of Lemma
20
.

Using the formula for interchanging covariant derivatives,

\displaystyle D_{t}D_{q}D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}=

\displaystyle D_{q}D_{t}D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}-R{(J^{q},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}

\displaystyle D_{t}D_{t}D_{q}D_{r}J^{s}=

\displaystyle D_{t}D_{q}D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}-D_{t}{(R{(J^{q},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}J^{s})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle D_{q}D_{t}D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}-R{(J^{q},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}-D_{t}{(R{(J^{q},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}J^{s})}

We will bound the norms of the above terms one by one

Bounding
D_{q}D_{t}D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}
:

From the proof of Lemma
19
,

\displaystyle D_{t}D_{t}D_{r}J^{s}=

\displaystyle-D_{r}{(R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime})}-R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}-D_{t}{(R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{(D_{r}R)}{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{s},D_{r}\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}

\displaystyle\quad-{(D_{t}R)}{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(D_{t}J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{r},D_{t}\gamma^{\prime})}J^{s}

\displaystyle\quad-R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{t}J^{s}

We will take derivative of each of these terms wrt
q
.

\displaystyle D_{q}{({(D_{r}R)}{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime})}=

\displaystyle{(D_{q}D_{r}R)}{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad+{(D_{r}R)}{(D_{q}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad+{(D_{r}R)}{(J^{s},D_{q}\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad+{(D_{r}R)}{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{q}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}{({(D_{r}R)}{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime})}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|D_{q}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{q}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+96L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+8L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+8L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96{(L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+1)})}

note:
L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}
is dimensionless,
L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}
is dimensionless,
L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{4}
is dimensionless.

\displaystyle D_{q}{(R{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime})}=

\displaystyle{(D_{q}R)}{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(D_{q}D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(D_{r}J^{s},D_{q}\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{q}\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}{(R{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}\gamma^{\prime})}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|D_{q}D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}

Lemma 21
.

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{s}E_{i}(s,t)\right\|}\leq 2L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}

21
.

stuff

and Lemma

. Integrating wrt

\displaystyle 104{(L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+1)})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 104{(L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R}^{\prime})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle D_{q}{(R{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}=

\displaystyle{(D_{q}R)}{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(D_{q}D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(D_{r}J^{s},D_{q}\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{q}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}{(R{(D_{r}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|D_{q}D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{q}\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|D_{r}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}{\left\|D_{q}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96L_{R}^{\prime}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{14}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{(L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+96L_{R}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+96L_{R}^{2}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{15}{(L_{R}+L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{14}L_{R}L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

Where we use Lemma
20
.

\displaystyle D_{q}{(R{(J^{s},D_{r}\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}=

\displaystyle{(D_{q}R)}{(J^{s},D_{r}\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(D_{q}J^{s},D_{r}\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(J^{s},D_{q}D_{r}\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(J^{s},D_{r}\gamma^{\prime})}D_{q}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}{(R{(J^{s},D_{r}\gamma^{\prime})}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{r}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|D_{q}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{r}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{q}D_{t}J^{r}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{r}\right\|}{\left\|D_{q}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+96L_{R}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+192L_{R}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}+8L_{R}^{2}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+8L_{R}^{2}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|s\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 304{(L_{R}^{\prime}+L_{R}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

where we use the fact that

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}D_{t}J^{r}\right\|}=

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}D_{q}J^{r}-R{(\gamma^{\prime},J^{q})}J^{r}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}+4L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}

\displaystyle D_{q}{(R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}=

\displaystyle{(D_{q}R)}{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(D_{q}J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(J^{s},D_{q}\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle\quad+R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{q}D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}{(R{(J^{s},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}{\left\|D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|D_{q}J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}{\left\|D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|D_{t}J^{q}\right\|}{\left\|D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+L_{R}{\left\|J^{s}\right\|}{\left\|\gamma^{\prime}\right\|}{\left\|D_{q}D_{r}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8L_{R}^{\prime}L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+96L_{R}^{2}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+8L_{R}^{2}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+208L_{R}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+1)})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 300{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

By symmetry, we can also bound

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}{(R{(J^{r},\gamma^{\prime})}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}\right\|}\leq 300{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

Summing,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}D_{t}D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle 104{(L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R}^{\prime})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{15}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+2^{14}L_{R}L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+304{(L_{R}^{\prime}+L_{R}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+300{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\quad+300{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{16}{({(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}+L_{R}^{\prime}+L_{R}L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

Finally,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}D_{q}D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}=

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}D_{t}D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}-R{(J^{q},\gamma^{\prime})}{(D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}D_{t}D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}+{\left\|R{(J^{q},\gamma^{\prime})}{(D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}\right\|}

We can bound

\displaystyle{\left\|R{(J^{q}J^{t})}{(D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)})}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{R}{\left\|z\right\|}{\left\|u\right\|}{\left\|D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 416L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+1)})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 832L_{R}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

Therefore, combining the above results and integrating wrt
t\in[0,1]
,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{q}D_{r}D_{s}E_{i}{(q,r,s,t)}\right\|}\leq

\displaystyle 2^{16}{({(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}+L_{R})}{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{2}+L_{R}{\left\|u\right\|})}+L_{R}^{\prime}+L_{R}L_{R}^{\prime\prime}{\left\|u\right\|}^{3})}{\left\|v\right\|}{\left\|w\right\|}{\left\|z\right\|}

∎

Appendix D
Tail Bounds

D.1
One-Step Distance Bounds

D.1.1
Under Lipschitz Continuity

Lemma 24

(One-step distance evolution under Lipschitz Continuity)
.

Let
\beta
be a vector field satisfying
2
. Assume in addition that
\delta\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
satisfies
\delta\leq{\frac{1}{16{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}}
. Let
L_{0}:={\left\|\beta(x_{0})\right\|}
. Let
x_{k}
be the following stochastic process:

\displaystyle x_{k+1}=\Exp_{x_{k}}{(\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))}

Then for any positive integer
K
, we can bound,

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x_{0})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle{(1+8\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2K}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+2\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}\leq\frac{1}{\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}\right\}}{(-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{})}

Proof.

We will be using the bound from
Zhang and Sra 2016
(see Lemma
41
). Let
v:=\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k})
. In case the minimizing geodesic from
x_{k}
to
x^{*}
is not unique, we let
\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})
be any arbitrary (but consistently fixed) choice. Then Lemma
41
bounds

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x)}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}-2\bm{\left\langle}v,\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}+{\tc{(\sqrt{L_{R}}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})})}}{\left\|v\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}-2\bm{\left\langle}v,\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}+{(1+\sqrt{L_{R}}\mathrm{d}(x_{k},x_{0}))}{\left\|v\right\|}^{2}

(D.1)

where
\zeta(r):=\frac{r}{\tanh(r)}
.

We will consider two cases:

Case 1:
\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}\leq\frac{1}{\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}
.

From (
D.1
):

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}-2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}+{(1+\sqrt{L_{R}}\mathrm{d}(x_{k},x_{0}))}{\left\|\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}-2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}+\delta^{2}{({L_{0}}^{2}+{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2})}+\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{2}\sqrt{L_{R}}{(L_{0}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}+{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{3})}+\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{\left\|{\xi_{k}(x_{k})}\right\|}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{4K}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}+\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{,}x_{0})}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}+\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+{\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1+3\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+\frac{1}{4K}+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+{(2K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}+\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+{\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}})}

where the third inequality uses the definition of Case 1, and the fourth inequality is by several applications of Young’s Inequality.

Case 2:
\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}>\frac{1}{4\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}
.

Let us define

\displaystyle z(t):=\Exp_{x_{k}}{(t{(\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))})}

I.e.
z(t)
interpolates between
x_{k}
and
x_{k+1}
. We verify that
z^{\prime}(t)=\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))}
. We also verify that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle-2\bm{\left\langle}\Exp^{-1}_{z(t)}(x_{0}),z^{\prime}(t)\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{-2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(z(t)),\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}}_{\hbox to9.22pt{\vbox to9.22pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.60942pt\lower-4.60942pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {

}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(z(t))-\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\delta\beta(x_{k}))},\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}}_{\hbox to9.22pt{\vbox to9.22pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.60942pt\lower-4.60942pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.99306pt}{-2.25555pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}-\underbrace{2\bm{\left\langle}\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))},\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}}_{\hbox to9.22pt{\vbox to9.22pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.60942pt\lower-4.60942pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.1 0 C 6.1 3.37 3.37 6.1 0 6.1 C -3.37 6.1 -6.1 3.37 -6.1 0 C -6.1 -3.37 -3.37 -6.1 0 -6.1 C 3.37 -6.1 6.1 -3.37 6.1 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.99306pt}{-2.25555pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}

Let’s upper bound the terms one by one.

We first bound
2
, which represents the "discretization error in drift":

\displaystyle\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}:=

\displaystyle 2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(z(t))-\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\delta\beta(x_{k}))},\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2{\left\|\delta\beta(z(t))-\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\delta\beta(x_{k}))}\right\|}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{k})}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{k})}^{2}+2\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}^{2}

By definition of
z(t)
, we know that
\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{k})}\leq{\left\|\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}\leq\delta L_{0}+\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}+\sqrt{\delta}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}
, so that
2\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{k})}^{2}\leq 8\delta^{3}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{3}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+8\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+8\delta^{3}L_{\beta}^{\prime}L_{0}^{2}\leq\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}
, so that

\displaystyle\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\leq

\displaystyle\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{k})}^{2}+\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

Next, we bound
3
, which is the most significant error term. From the definition of Case 2,
\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}>\frac{1}{\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}
,

\displaystyle\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\leq

\displaystyle 2\bm{\left\langle}\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))},\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{\delta}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}^{2}+\frac{1}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}^{2}+{\delta L_{R}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}

where we use our assumption that
\delta\leq\frac{1}{{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}
.

Finally, we bound
1
as

\displaystyle-2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(z(t)),\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{}\leq

\displaystyle 4\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}^{2}+4K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{4K}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}^{2}

Putting everything together,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle{(6\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{4K})}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{0})}^{2}+{(\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+4K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

By Gronwall’s Lemma (integrating from
t=0
to
t=1
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(z(1),x_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp{(6\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{4K})}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+{(2\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+2\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+2\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1+8\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2K}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+2\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

where we use the assumption that
\delta\leq\frac{1}{8L_{\beta}^{\prime}}
.

Combining Case 1 and Case 2:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x_{0})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle{(1+8\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2K}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+2\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}\leq\frac{1}{\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}\right\}}{(-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{})}

∎

D.1.2
Under Dissipativity

Lemma 25

(One-step distance evolution under Dissipativity)
.

Assume
\beta
satisfies
2
. Let
x^{*}
be some point with
\beta(x^{*})=0
. Assume that for all
x
such that
\mathrm{d}{(x,x^{*})}\geq\mathcal{R}
, there exists a minimizing geodesic
\gamma:[0,1]\to M
with
\gamma(0)=x,\gamma(1)=x^{*}
, and

\displaystyle\bm{\left\langle}\beta(x),\gamma^{\prime}(0)\bm{}\leq-m\mathrm{d}{(x,x^{*})}^{2}

. Assume in addition that
\delta\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
satisfies
\delta\leq{\frac{m}{128{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}}

Let
x_{k}
be the following stochastic process:

\displaystyle x_{k+1}=\Exp_{x_{k}}{(\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))}

Then

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x^{*})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle{(1-\delta m)}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+{\frac{2048\delta L_{R}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{4}}{m^{5}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{4}+4\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}\leq\frac{m}{4\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}\right\}}{(-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{})}

Proof.

Throughout the proof, it is useful to note that by our assumptions, it must be that
m\leq L_{\beta}^{\prime}
. We will be using the bound from
Zhang and Sra 2016
(see Lemma
41
). Let
v:=\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k})
. In case the minimizing geodesic from
x_{k}
to
x^{*}
is not unique, we let
\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})
be any arbitrary (but consistently fixed) choice that satisfies
\bm{\left\langle}\beta(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}\leq-m\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}
. Then Lemma
41
bounds

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x)}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}-2\bm{\left\langle}v,\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}+{\tc{(\sqrt{L_{R}}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})})}}{\left\|v\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}-2\bm{\left\langle}v,\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}+{(1+\sqrt{L_{R}}\mathrm{d}(x_{k},x^{*}))}{\left\|v\right\|}^{2}

(D.2)

where
\zeta(r):=\frac{r}{\tanh(r)}
.

We will consider two cases:

Case 1:
\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}\leq\frac{m}{4\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}
.

From (
D.2
):

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}^{2}{(x_{k+1},x^{*})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}^{2}{(x_{k},x^{*})}-2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}+{(1+\sqrt{L_{R}}\mathrm{d}(x_{k},x^{*}))}{\left\|\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}^{2}{(x_{k},x^{*})}-2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}+\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{2}\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{3}+\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{\left\|{\xi_{k}(x_{k})}\right\|}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1+\delta m/2)}\mathrm{d}^{2}{(x_{k},x^{*})}-2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}+\frac{4\delta L_{R}}{m}{\left\|{\xi_{k}(x_{k})}\right\|}^{4}

(D.3)

where we use our assumptions that
\delta\leq m/{(16{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2})}
and the inequality under Case 1. We used Cauchy Schwarz a few times.

We can further bound

\displaystyle 2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}\geq\mathcal{R}\right\}}{(-2m\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2})}+{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}\leq\mathcal{R}\right\}}{(2L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-2\delta m\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+2\delta{(m+L_{\beta}^{\prime})}\mathcal{R}^{2}

Thus

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}^{2}{(x_{k+1},x^{*})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle{(1-\delta m)}\mathrm{d}^{2}{(x_{k},x^{*})}-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+2\delta{(m+L_{\beta}^{\prime})}\mathcal{R}^{2}+\frac{4\delta L_{R}}{m}{\left\|{\xi_{k}(x_{k})}\right\|}^{4}

(D.4)

where we use the fact that
e^{\delta m}\leq e^{\frac{m^{2}}{16{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}}\leq 2
.

Case 2:
\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}>\frac{m}{4\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}
.

Let us define

\displaystyle z(t):=\Exp_{x_{k}}{(t{(\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))})}

I.e.
z(t)
interpolates between
x_{k}
and
x_{k+1}
. We verify that
z^{\prime}(t)=\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))}
. We also verify that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle-2\bm{\left\langle}\Exp^{-1}_{z(t)}(x^{*}),z^{\prime}(t)\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{-2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(z(t)),\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}}_{\hbox to9.22pt{\vbox to9.22pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.60942pt\lower-4.60942pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {

}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.99306pt}{-2.25555pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(z(t))-\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\delta\beta(x_{k}))},\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}}_{\hbox to9.22pt{\vbox to9.22pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.60942pt\lower-4.60942pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.1 0 C 6.1 3.37 3.37 6.1 0 6.1 C -3.37 6.1 -6.1 3.37 -6.1 0 C -6.1 -3.37 -3.37 -6.1 0 -6.1 C 3.37 -6.1 6.1 -3.37 6.1 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.99306pt}{-2.25555pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}-\underbrace{2\bm{\left\langle}\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))},\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}}_{\hbox to9.22pt{\vbox to9.22pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.60942pt\lower-4.60942pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.1 0 C 6.1 3.37 3.37 6.1 0 6.1 C -3.37 6.1 -6.1 3.37 -6.1 0 C -6.1 -3.37 -3.37 -6.1 0 -6.1 C 3.37 -6.1 6.1 -3.37 6.1 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-1.99306pt}{-2.25555pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}

Let’s upper bound the terms one by one.

We first bound
2
, which represents the "discretization error in drift":

\displaystyle\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}:=

\displaystyle 2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(z(t))-\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\delta\beta(x_{k}))},\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2{\left\|\delta\beta(z(t))-\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\delta\beta(x_{k}))}\right\|}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{k})}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\delta m}{4}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{4\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}{m}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{k})}^{2}

By definition of
z(t)
, we know that
\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{k})}\leq{\left\|\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}\leq\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}+\sqrt{\delta}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}
, so that
\frac{4\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}{m}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x_{k})}^{2}\leq\frac{4\delta^{3}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{4}}{m}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{4\delta^{2}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}{m}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}\leq\frac{\delta m}{8}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}
, so that

\displaystyle\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\delta m}{4}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{\delta m}{8}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}

Next, we bound
3
, which is the most significant error term. From the definition of Case 2,
\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}>\frac{1}{4\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}
,

\displaystyle\hbox to11.37pt{\vbox to11.37pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 5.68657pt\lower-5.68657pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {
{{}}\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 7.59 0 C 7.59 4.19 4.19 7.59 0 7.59 C -4.19 7.59 -7.59 4.19 -7.59 0 C -7.59 -4.19 -4.19 -7.59 0 -7.59 C 4.19 -7.59 7.59 -4.19 7.59 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-2.5pt}{-3.22221pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform} \pgfsys@hbox{60}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
}
\lxSVG@closescope {{{}}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}\leq

\displaystyle 2\bm{\left\langle}\Gamma_{z(0)}^{z(t)}{(\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))},\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{\delta}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\delta m}{8}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{8}{m}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\delta m}{8}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{32\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}{m^{2}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\delta m}{8}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{\delta m}{8}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{2048\delta L_{R}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{4}}{m^{5}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{4}

where we use our assumption that
\delta\leq\frac{m}{128{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{2}}
.

Finally, we bound
1
as

\displaystyle-2\bm{\left\langle}\delta\beta(z(t)),\Exp_{z(t)}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2}\geq\mathcal{R}\right\}}{(-2\delta m\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2})}+{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}\leq\mathcal{R}\right\}}{(2\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathrm{d}{(z(t)x^{*})})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}\mathcal{R}^{2}-2\delta m\mathrm{d}{(\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2})}

Putting everything together,

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{3\delta m}{2}\mathrm{d}{(z(t),x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{\delta m}{4}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{2048\delta L_{R}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{4}}{m^{5}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{4}+4\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{2}

By Gronwall’s Lemma (integrating from
t=0
to
t=1
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x^{*})}^{2}=

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(z(1),x^{*})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp{(-{3\delta m/2})}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{\delta m}{4}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{2048\delta L_{R}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{4}}{m^{5}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{4}+4\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1-\delta m)}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+\frac{2048\delta L_{R}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{4}}{m^{5}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{4}+4\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{2}

(D.5)

where we use the assumption that
\delta\leq\frac{1}{128m}
so that
\exp{(-3\delta m/2)}\leq 1-5\delta m/4
.

Combining Case 1 and Case 2:

Combining (
D.4
) and (
D.5
),

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x^{*})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle{(1-\delta m)}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}^{2}+{(\frac{2048\delta L_{R}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}^{4}}{m^{5}}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{4}+4\delta{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}\mathcal{R}^{2})}

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x^{*})}\leq\frac{1}{4\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}\right\}}{(-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x^{*})\bm{})}

∎

D.2
L_{p}
Bounds

D.2.1
Under Lipschitz Continuity

Lemma 26

(L2 Bound and Chevyshev under Lipschitz Continuity)
.

Consider the same setup as Lemma
24
.

Assume in addition that there exists
\sigma_{\xi}\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
such that for all
x
and for all
k
,
\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|\xi_{k}(x)\right\|}^{2}}\right]\leq\sigma_{\xi}^{2}
. Then for any positive integer
K
, and for all
k\leq K
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}}\right]\leq\exp{(1+8K\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+K\delta L_{R}\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+K\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}\cdot{(2K\delta\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+8K^{2}\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2})}

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}{({\max_{k\leq K}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}\geq s})}\leq\frac{1}{s}\exp{(2+8K\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+K\delta L_{R}\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+K\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}\cdot{(2K\delta\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+4K^{2}\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2})}

Proof.

Let
{\mathcal{F}}_{k}
denote the
\sigma
-field generated by
\xi_{0}...\xi_{k-1}
.

To bound the first claim, take expectation of the bound from Lemma
24
wrt
{\mathcal{F}}_{k}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{{\mathcal{F}}_{k}}}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x_{0})}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{{\mathcal{F}}_{k}}}\left[{{(1+8\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2K}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}}}\right]+2\delta\mathbb{E}_{{{\mathcal{F}}_{k}}}\left[{{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}}\right]+8K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{{{\mathcal{F}}_{k}}}\left[{{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}\leq\frac{1}{\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}\right\}}{(-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{})}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(1+8\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2K}+\delta L_{R}\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}}+2\delta\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp{(8\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2K}+\delta L_{R}\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}}+2\delta\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

Applying the above recursively,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(x_{K},x_{0})}^{2}}\right]\leq\exp{(1+8K\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+K\delta L_{R}\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+K\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}\cdot{(2K\delta\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+8K^{2}\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2})}

The above upper bound clearly also holds for
\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}}\right]
for all
k\leq K
. This proves our first claim.

To prove the second claim, let us define

\displaystyle r_{0}^{2}:=0

\displaystyle r_{k+1}^{2}:={(1+8\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{4K}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}r_{k}^{2}+2\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+{\mathbbm{1}\left\{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}\leq\frac{1}{\delta\sqrt{L_{R}}{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}}\right\}}{(-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}),\Exp_{x_{k}}^{-1}(x_{0})\bm{})}

We verify that
r_{k}
as defined above is a sub-martingale. Thus by Doob’s martingale inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}{({\max_{k\leq K}r_{k}^{2}\geq s})}\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[{r_{K}^{2}}\right]}{s}

Furthermore, notice that

\displaystyle r_{0}^{2}=\mathrm{d}{(x_{0},x_{0})}^{2}=0

\displaystyle r_{k+1}^{2}-\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1}^{2},x_{0})}^{2}\geq{(1+8\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{2}{K}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}{(r_{k}^{2}-\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2})}\geq 0

so that
r_{k}\geq\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}
with probability 1, for all
k
.

Thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}{({\max_{k\leq K}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}\geq s})}\leq\mathbb{P}{({\max_{k\leq K}r_{k}^{2}\geq s})}\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[{r_{K}^{2}}\right]}{s}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{s}\exp{(1+8K\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+K\delta L_{R}\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+K\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}\cdot{(2K\delta\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+8K^{2}\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2})}

The proof for the bound on
r_{K}^{2}
is identical to the proof of the first claim. We conclude our proof of the second claim
∎

Lemma 27

(L4 Bound and Chevyshev under Lipschitz Continuity)
.

Let
\beta
be a vector field satisfying
2
. Assume in addition that
\delta\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
satisfies
\delta\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{16{L_{\beta}^{\prime}}},\frac{1}{16\sqrt{L_{R}}L_{0}},\frac{1}{L_{R}d}\right\}
. Let
L_{0}:={\left\|\beta(x_{0})\right\|}
. Let
x_{k}
be the following stochastic process:

\displaystyle x_{k+1}=\Exp_{x_{k}}{(\delta\beta(x_{k})+\sqrt{\delta}\xi_{k}(x_{k}))}

Assume in addition that there exists
\sigma_{\xi}\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
such that for all
x
and for all
k
,
\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|\xi_{k}(x)\right\|}^{4}}\right]\leq 2d^{2}
. Then for any positive
K\geq 4
, and for all
k\leq K
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{4}}\right]\leq\exp{(2+8K\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+K\delta L_{R}\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+K\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}\cdot{(2K\delta\sigma_{\xi}^{2}+4K^{2}\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2})}

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}{({\max_{k\leq K}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}\geq s})}\leq\frac{1}{s^{4}}\exp{(2+16K\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+4K\delta L_{R}d+3K\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}{(5K^{2}\delta^{2}d^{2}+64K^{4}\delta^{4}L_{0}^{4})}

Proof.

Let
{\mathcal{F}}_{k}
denote the
\sigma
-field generated by
\xi_{0}...\xi_{k-1}
.

We will use the following inequality from Lemma
24
:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}{(x_{k+1},x_{0})}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle{(1+8\delta L_{\beta}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2K}+\delta L_{R}{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+\delta^{2}L_{R}L_{0}^{2})}\mathrm{d}{(x_{k},x_{0})}^{2}+2\delta{\left\|\xi_{k}(x_{k})\right\|}^{2}+8K\delta^{2}L_{0}^{2}
