\displaystyle\delta{\left\|{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})\right\|}_{2}^{2}-2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},\delta\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\gamma_{3}\bm{}+{\left\|\delta\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\gamma_{3}\right\|}_{2}^{2}

Using the upper bounds on
\gamma_{3}
, we verify that

\displaystyle{\left|2\bm{\left\langle}\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},\delta\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\gamma_{3}\bm{}\right|}\leq

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}\cdot{\left\|\delta\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\gamma_{3}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}

\displaystyle{\left\|\delta\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\gamma_{3}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

Plugging the above into (
E.6
) gives our third bound

\displaystyle{\left|{\left\|{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-{({\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+2\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},-\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})+{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\delta{\left\|{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})\right\|}_{2}^{2})}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2})}

∎

Lemma 49
.

Let
{\mathbf{H}}
satisfy (
E.4
). Let
\delta\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{16L_{R}L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}},\frac{1}{16{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{2}}\right\}
. Let
{\mathcal{G}}(\mathbf{u}):=\mathbf{u}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})
. Assume
\bar{\mathbf{u}}
satisfies
{\left\|\bar{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{L_{R}}}
, and let
\mathbf{u}:={\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}
. Then

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}_{2}-I\right\|}\leq\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-{(I+\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\right\|}_{2}\leq\delta L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-{(I+\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}\right\|}_{2}\leq\delta^{3/2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}

Proof of Lemma
49
.

We define the line
\ell(t)=t(\hat{\mathbf{u}})+(1-t){\mathcal{G}}^{-1}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})
. Using identical steps as the proof of Lemma
46
, we can show that
{\left\|\ell(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{L_{R}}}
for all
t\in[0,1]
. Therefore, the Lipschitz derivative bounds for
{\mathbf{H}}
in (
C.5
) hold along
\ell(t)
. Note that
\hat{\mathbf{u}}=\ell(1)
and
\mathbf{u}=\ell(0)
.

We will define three levels of increasingly finer approximations for
\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}
.

By definition of
{\mathcal{G}}
,

\displaystyle\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}=

\displaystyle I+\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}

The first level of approximation is thus

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-I\right\|}_{2}\leq\sqrt{\delta}{\left\|\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\right\|}_{2}\leq\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}

Next, let us define

\displaystyle\nu:=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}-\hat{\mathbf{u}}

From Lemma
47
,
{\left\|\nu\right\|}_{2}\leq\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}
. Using a first-order expansion of
\nabla{\mathbf{H}}
,

\displaystyle\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}+\nu)}=\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}+t\nu)},\nu\bm{}dt

Therefore,

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-{(I+\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\right\|}_{2}=

\displaystyle{\left\|\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\sqrt{\delta}\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}+t\nu)},\nu\bm{}dt\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}

Finally, let us define

\displaystyle\varepsilon:=

\displaystyle{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}-{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}-\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}}))}

From Lemma
47
,
{\left\|\varepsilon\right\|}_{2}\leq\delta L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}
. Note that by our definition,
\nu=-\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})+\varepsilon
.

Performing a second-order Taylor expansion of
\nabla{\mathbf{H}}
,

\displaystyle\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}+\nu)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}+t\nu)},\nu\bm{}dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},\nu\bm{}+\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{3}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}+s\nu)}\nu ds,\nu\bm{}dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}-\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}

\displaystyle\quad+\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},\varepsilon\bm{}+\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{3}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}+s\nu)}\nu ds,\nu\bm{}dt

Using our earlier bound on
{\left\|\varepsilon\right\|}_{2}
and
{\left\|\nu\right\|}_{2}
,

\displaystyle{\left\|\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},\varepsilon\bm{}\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\right\|}_{2}{\left\|\varepsilon\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle{\left\|\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{3}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}+s\nu)}\nu ds,\nu\bm{}dt\right\|}_{2}\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla^{3}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\right\|}_{2}{\left\|\nu\right\|}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}

Thus

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-{(I+\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-{(\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\delta}{\left\|\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},\varepsilon\bm{}+\int_{0}^{1}\bm{\left\langle}\int_{0}^{t}\nabla^{3}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}}+s\nu)}\nu ds,\nu\bm{}dt\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}

∎

Lemma 50
.

Let
{\mathbf{H}}
satisfy (
E.4
). Let
\delta\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{16L_{R}L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}},\frac{1}{16{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{2}}\right\}
. Let
{\mathcal{G}}(\mathbf{u}):=\mathbf{u}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})
. Assume
\bar{\mathbf{u}}
satisfies
{\left\|\bar{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{L_{R}}}
, and let
\mathbf{u}:={\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}
. Then

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})})}-{(\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-\delta\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}-\frac{\delta}{2}\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})})}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})})}-\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\right|}\leq

\displaystyle\delta d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2})}

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})})}\right|}\leq\sqrt{\delta}\cdot 2dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}

Proof.

From Lemma
49
,

\displaystyle\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}=I+\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3}

for some
\mu_{3}
satisfying

\displaystyle{\left\|\mu_{3}+\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mu_{3}-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mu_{3}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{3/2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}

Let
A:=\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-I=\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3}
.

We verify that

\displaystyle\text{tr}{(A)}=\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-\delta\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}+\text{tr}{(\mu_{3})}

and

\displaystyle\text{tr}{(A^{2})}=

\displaystyle\delta\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})}+\text{tr}{({(\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}{(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})})}

\displaystyle\quad+\text{tr}{({(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})}^{2})}

By Cauchy Schwarz,

\displaystyle{\left|\text{tr}{({(\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}{(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})})}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\text{tr}{({(\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}{(\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{T})}}\cdot\sqrt{\text{tr}{({(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})}{(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})}^{T})}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d{\left\|\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\right\|}_{2}{\left\|-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}

Again by Cauchy Schwarz,

\displaystyle\text{tr}{({(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})}^{2})}\leq

\displaystyle\text{tr}{({(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})}{(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})}^{T})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

Finally,
{\left|\text{tr}{(\mu_{3})}\right|}\leq d{\left\|\mu_{3}\right\|}_{2}=\delta^{3/2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}
.

From Lemma
59
,

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(I+A)}-{(\text{tr}{(A)}-\frac{\text{tr}{(A^{2})}}{2})}\right|}\leq 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{3}

(E.7)

By plugging the previous bounds into (
E.7
), and moving terms around,

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(I+A)}-{(\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-\delta\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}-\frac{\delta}{2}\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})})}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\left|\text{tr}{(\mu_{3})}\right|}+\frac{1}{2}{\left|\text{tr}{({(\sqrt{\delta}\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}{(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})})}+\text{tr}{({(-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\mu_{3})}^{2})}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\delta^{3/2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}

This proves our first claim.

We note that

\displaystyle{\left|\text{tr}{(A)}-\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\right|}\leq d{\left\|\mu_{3}-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}\right\|}_{2}\leq\delta dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}

Combining the above with the bound
{\left|\log\det{(I+A)}-\text{tr}{(A)}\right|}\leq 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{2}
from Lemma
59
, we get

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(I+A)}-\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\right|}\leq

\displaystyle 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\delta L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2})}

This proves our second claim.

Finally, using the third inequality of Lemma
59
,

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(I+A)}\right|}\leq 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}=2d\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}

∎

Lemma 51
.

Let
x\in\mathbb{R}
be a random variable satisfying
\mathbb{E}\left[{x}\right]=0
and
{\left|x\right|}\leq\alpha\leq\frac{1}{2}
almost surely. Then

\displaystyle{\left|\mathbb{E}\left[{e^{x}}\right]-e^{1/2\mathbb{E}\left[{x^{2}}\right]}\right|}\leq 8\alpha^{3}

Proof of Lemma
51
.

By Taylor’s Theorem

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{e^{x}}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{1+x+\frac{x^{2}}{2}+\sum_{i=3}^{\infty}\frac{x^{i}}{i!}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{1+\frac{x^{2}}{2}+\sum_{i=3}^{\infty}\frac{x^{i}}{i!}}\right]

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\exp{(\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{x^{2}}\right])}=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{1+\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[{x^{2}}\right]}{2}+\sum_{i=2}^{\infty}\frac{{(\mathbb{E}\left[{x^{2}}\right]/2)}^{i}}{i!}}\right]

We verify that

\displaystyle{\left|\mathbb{E}\left[{\sum_{i=3}^{\infty}\frac{x^{i}}{i!}}\right]\right|}=

\displaystyle{\left|\mathbb{E}\left[{x^{3}\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\frac{x^{i}}{{(i+3)}!}}\right]\right|}\leq\alpha^{3}\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\frac{\alpha^{i}}{{i}!}\leq\alpha^{3}e^{\alpha}

and that

\displaystyle{\left|\sum_{i=2}^{\infty}\frac{{(\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{x^{2}}\right])}^{i}}{i!}\right|}=

\displaystyle{\left|\sum_{i=2}^{\infty}\frac{{(\mathbb{E}\left[{x^{2}}\right]/2)}^{i}}{i!}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{4}}{8}{\left|\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\frac{{(\mathbb{E}\left[{x^{2}}\right]/2)}^{i}}{{(i+2)}!}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{4}}{8}e^{\alpha^{2}/2}

Using our assumption that
\alpha\leq 1
, we know that
\alpha^{4}\leq\alpha^{3}
and
\alpha^{2}\leq\alpha\leq 1
, so that

\displaystyle\alpha^{3}e^{\alpha}+\frac{\alpha^{4}}{8}e^{\alpha^{2}/2}\leq 8\alpha^{3}

∎

Lemma 52
.

Consider the same setup as Lemma
43
with
\mathbf{u}_{0},\mathbf{u}_{\delta},\hat{\mathbf{u}}_{\delta}
as defined in (
E.3
), with densities
p_{0},p_{\delta},\hat{p}_{\delta}
respectively. Let
{\mathcal{G}}(\mathbf{u}):=\mathbf{u}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})
. Assume
\delta\leq\frac{1}{16d^{2}{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{2}}
. For any
r\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
, for any
{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{4\sqrt{L_{R}}},r\sqrt{k\delta}\right\}
, and for any
k\geq\max\left\{256r^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2},16L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}\right\}
,

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{\delta}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}=p_{0}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{(\exp{(-\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{2k^{2}\delta})}+32\varepsilon)}

for some
{\left|\varepsilon\right|}\leq 32{\mathcal{C}}_{k}
, where
{\mathcal{C}}_{k}
is as defined in (
E.11
).

Proof of Lemma
52
.

From Lemma
46
, and noting that
p_{0}(\mathbf{u})\propto\exp{(-\frac{1}{2}{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}_{2}^{2})}
,

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{\delta}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}=

\displaystyle p_{0}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\mathbb{E}\left[{\exp{(-\frac{1}{2k\delta}{({\left\|{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2})}-\log\det{(\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})})})}}\right]

For convenience, define
\alpha:=-\frac{1}{2}{({\left\|{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2})}
and
\beta:=-\log\det{(\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})})}
.

Using series expansion of
e^{x}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{p}_{\delta}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{p_{0}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}=\mathbb{E}\left[{\exp{(\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta)}}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\frac{{(\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta)}^{i}}{i!}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{1+\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta+\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2k^{2}\delta^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}\beta}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[{\sum_{i=3}^{\infty}\frac{{(\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta)}^{i}}{i!}}\right]

(E.8)

We first consider
\mathbb{E}\left[{\alpha}\right]
.

Let us define

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{3}:=-\frac{1}{2}{\left\|{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}{({\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-2\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})\bm{}+\delta\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\delta{\left\|{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})\right\|}_{2}^{2})}

From Lemma
48
,
{\left|\varepsilon_{3}\right|}\leq\delta^{3/2}{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2})}

Using the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}\right]=0
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\alpha}\right]=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\delta\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\delta{\left\|{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\varepsilon_{3}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\delta}{2}\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},\mathbb{E}\left[{\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}\right]\bm{}-\frac{\delta d}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\varepsilon_{3}}\right]

Where we use the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}}){\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})^{T}}\right]=I
.

We next consider
\mathbb{E}\left[{\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}}\right]
.

Let us define

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{2}:=-\frac{1}{2}{\left\|{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}{({\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-2\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}

From Lemma
48
,
{\left|\varepsilon_{2}\right|}\leq\delta{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}
.
Note that
\alpha=\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\varepsilon_{2}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\alpha^{2}}\right]=

\displaystyle\delta\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}^{2}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[{\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}+\varepsilon_{2}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left[{\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}+\varepsilon_{2}^{2}}\right]

where we use the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}^{T}}\right]=I
. The last two terms can be bounded as

\displaystyle{\left|\mathbb{E}\left[{\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}+\varepsilon_{2}^{2}}\right]\right|}\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}{(8L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+4L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+\sqrt{\delta}{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}^{2})}

Next, we consider
\mathbb{E}\left[{\beta}\right]
.

Define

\displaystyle\tau_{3}:=-\log\det{(\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})})}+{(\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}-\delta\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}-\frac{\delta}{2}\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})})}

so that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\beta}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{-\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}+\delta\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}+\frac{\delta}{2}\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[{\tau_{3}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\delta\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}+\frac{\delta}{2}\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[{\tau_{3}}\right]

where we use the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left[{-\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}}\right]=-\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}\right])}=0
.

From the first inequality of Lemma
50
,

\displaystyle{\left|\tau_{3}\right|}\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}

Next, we consider
\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\beta^{2}}\right]

We define

\displaystyle\tau_{2}:=-\log\det{(\nabla{\mathcal{G}}{({\mathcal{G}}^{-1}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})})}+\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}

Expanding the quadratic,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\beta^{2}}\right]=\delta\mathbb{E}\left[{\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[{-\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\cdot\tau_{2}+\tau_{2}^{2}}\right]

The second term can be bounded with probability 1 as

\displaystyle{\left|-\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\cdot\tau_{2}+\tau_{2}^{2}\right|}\leq

\displaystyle{\left|\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\cdot\tau_{2}\right|}+{\left|\tau_{2}\right|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\left|\beta\right|}{\tau_{2}}+{\left|\tau_{2}\right|}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{\delta}\cdot 2dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}\cdot\delta d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2})}+{(\delta d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2})})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}d^{2}{(4L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+8{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3}+8\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+32\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{4})}

where we bound
{\left|\tau_{2}\right|}\leq\delta d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2})}
using the second inequality of Lemma
50
and
{\left|\beta\right|}\leq\sqrt{\delta}\cdot 2dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}
using the third inequality of Lemma
50
.

Finally, we consider
\mathbb{E}\left[{\alpha\beta}\right]
.

We will use

\displaystyle\alpha=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}+\varepsilon_{2}

\displaystyle\beta=

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\delta}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}+\tau_{2}

Thus

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\alpha\beta}\right]=

\displaystyle-\delta\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[{\varepsilon_{2}\cdot\beta+\tau_{2}\cdot\alpha-\tau_{2}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\delta\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}}\right]\bm{}+\mathbb{E}\left[{\varepsilon_{2}\cdot\beta+\tau_{2}\cdot\alpha-\tau_{2}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}}\right]

We can bound, with probability 1, the last term as

\displaystyle{\left|\varepsilon_{2}\cdot\beta\right|}+{\left|\tau_{2}\cdot\alpha\right|}+{\left|\tau_{2}\cdot\varepsilon_{2}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(\delta{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})})}\cdot{(\sqrt{\delta}\cdot 2dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\quad+{(\delta d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2})})}\cdot{(\sqrt{\delta}{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})})}

\displaystyle\quad+{(\delta{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})})}\cdot{(\delta d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+2{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2})})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}d{(8{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+2\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+4\sqrt{\delta}(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{3}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{3/2}d{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+4\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})})}

We now simplify
\mathbb{E}\left[{\alpha+\beta+\frac{1}{2}\alpha^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}+\alpha\beta}\right]

From the first claim in Lemma
58
, two terms from
\mathbb{E}\left[{\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[{\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}\beta}\right]
respectively cancel out:

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{k\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},\mathbb{E}\left[{\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}\right]\bm{}-\frac{1}{k\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\hat{\mathbf{u}},\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}}\right]\bm{}=0

From the second claim from Lemma
58
, three terms from
\mathbb{E}\left[{\beta}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[{\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}}\right]
cancel out:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{2\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}+\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})}+\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2}}\right]=0

Put together,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta+\frac{1}{2k^{2}\delta^{2}}\alpha^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}\beta}\right]=-\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{2k^{2}\delta}+\varepsilon

(E.9)

for some
{\left|\varepsilon\right|}\leq 32{\mathcal{C}}^{\prime}
, where

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}^{\prime}:=

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{k^{2}\sqrt{\delta}}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2})}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}{{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{3/2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+\delta^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{3/2}d^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+\delta^{2}{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{4})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}d{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{3}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{r\delta}{\sqrt{k}}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{r^{2}\sqrt{\delta}}{k}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{r}{k^{3/2}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{3/2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+\delta^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{3/2}d^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+\delta^{2}{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{4})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{r\delta}{\sqrt{k}}d{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}+\frac{r\delta^{3/2}}{\sqrt{k}}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{3}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}

From Lemma
48
,

\displaystyle{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}\right|}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{k\sqrt{\delta}}{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2rL_{{\mathbf{H}}}}{\sqrt{k}}+\frac{L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}}{k}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}

where the second inequality uses our bound that
{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}\leq r\sqrt{k\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}}
, and the last inequality uses the assumption that
k\geq 16L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}
and
k\geq 256r^{2}L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}
.

From Lemma
49
and Lemma
59
,
{\left|\beta\right|}\leq 2\sqrt{\delta}dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}\leq\frac{1}{8}
with probability 1. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}\leq\frac{1}{4}

and

\displaystyle{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta+\frac{1}{2}{(\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta)}^{2}\right|}\leq\frac{1}{2}

From our assumptions,
\frac{d}{2k}\leq\frac{1}{8}
and
\frac{{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{2k^{2}\delta}\leq\frac{r^{2}k\delta}{2k^{2}\delta}\leq\frac{r^{2}}{2k}\leq\frac{1}{8}
, so that by Taylor expansion,

\displaystyle{\left|{(1-\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{2k^{2}\delta})}-\exp{(-\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{2k^{2}\delta})}\right|}\leq\frac{d^{2}}{k^{2}}+\frac{r^{4}}{k^{2}}

(E.10)

We can bound the term
\mathbb{E}\left[{\sum_{i=3}^{\infty}\frac{{(\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta)}^{i}}{i!}}\right]
as

\displaystyle{\left|\mathbb{E}\left[{\sum_{i=3}^{\infty}\frac{{(\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta)}^{i}}{i!}}\right]\right|}\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\sum_{i=3}^{\infty}\frac{{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}^{i}}{i!}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}^{3}\cdot\sum_{i=3}^{\infty}\frac{{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}^{i-3}}{i!}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}^{3}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\frac{{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}^{i}}{i!}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}^{3}\cdot e^{{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}}}\right]

Using our earlier bound that
e^{{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}}\leq\exp{(\frac{1}{4})}
,

\displaystyle{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}^{3}\cdot e^{{\left|\frac{\alpha}{k\delta}+\beta\right|}}\leq

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}{(\frac{2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}+\sqrt{\delta}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}}{k\delta}+2dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3}\cdot\exp{(\frac{1}{4})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\delta^{3/2}{(\frac{L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{3}+\delta^{3/2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{6}}{k^{3}\delta^{3}}+d^{3}{L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8r^{3}\frac{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{3}}{k^{3/2}}+8\frac{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{6}}{k^{3}}+8\delta^{3/2}d^{3}{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{3}

Plugging the above and (
E.10
) into (
E.8
),

\displaystyle\hat{p}_{\delta}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}=p_{0}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{(\exp{(\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{2k})}+\varepsilon)}

for some
{\left|\varepsilon\right|}\leq 32{\mathcal{C}}_{k}{(1+r^{4})}
, where

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{k}:=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{k}}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{k^{3/2}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{3/2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+\delta^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{3/2}d^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+\delta^{2}{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{4})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{k}}d{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}+\frac{\delta^{3/2}}{\sqrt{k}}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{3}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{d^{2}}{k^{2}}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{3}}{k^{3/2}}+\frac{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{6}}{k^{3}}+\delta^{3/2}d^{3}{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{3}

(E.11)

∎

Lemma 53
.

Consider the same setup as Lemma
43
with
\mathbf{u}_{0},\mathbf{u}_{\delta},\hat{\mathbf{u}}_{\delta}
as defined in (
E.3
), with densities
p_{0},p_{\delta},\hat{p}_{\delta}
respectively. Let
{\mathcal{G}}(\mathbf{u}):=\mathbf{u}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})
. Assume
\delta\leq\frac{1}{16d^{2}{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{2}}
. For any
r\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
, for any
{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{4\sqrt{L_{R}}},r\sqrt{k\delta}\right\}
, and for any
k\geq\max\left\{256r^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2},16L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}\right\}
,

\displaystyle p_{\delta}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})=

\displaystyle p_{0}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\cdot\exp{(\frac{1}{2k^{2}\delta}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-\frac{d}{2k}+\varepsilon)}

for some
{\left|\varepsilon\right|}\leq\frac{2d}{k^{2}}
.

Proof of Lemma
53
.

Note that

\displaystyle p_{0}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})\propto\exp{(-\frac{1}{2k\delta}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-\frac{d}{2}\log{(k\delta)})}

\displaystyle p_{\delta}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})\propto\exp{(-\frac{1}{2(k+1)\delta}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-\frac{d}{2}\log{((k+1)\delta)})}

Reorganizing,

\displaystyle p_{\delta}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})=

\displaystyle p_{0}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\cdot\exp{(\frac{1}{2k\delta}{(1-\frac{k}{k+1})}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-\frac{d}{2}\log{(\frac{k+1}{k})})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle p_{0}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\cdot\exp{(\frac{1}{2k^{2}\delta}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-\frac{d}{2k}+\varepsilon)}

where
{\left|\varepsilon\right|}\leq\frac{2d}{k^{2}}
(Taylor expansion).
∎

The following Lemma is a restatement of Lemma
45
, for ease of reference

Lemma 54
.

Consider the same setup as Lemma
43
with
\mathbf{u}_{0},\mathbf{u}_{\delta},\hat{\mathbf{u}}_{\delta}
as defined in (
E.3
), with densities
p_{0},p_{\delta},\hat{p}_{\delta}
respectively. Let
{\mathcal{G}}(\mathbf{u}):=\mathbf{u}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})
. Assume
\delta\leq\frac{1}{16d^{2}{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{2}}
. For any
r\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
, for any
{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{4\sqrt{L_{R}}},r\sqrt{k\delta}\right\}
, and for any
k\geq\max\left\{256r^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2},16L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}\right\}
,

\displaystyle{\left|\frac{\hat{p}_{\delta}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}{p_{\delta}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}-1\right|}^{2}\leq 2^{12}{\mathcal{C}}_{k}^{2}{(1+r^{8})}+\frac{16d^{2}}{k^{4}}

where
{\mathcal{C}}_{k}
is defined in (
E.11
).

Proof of Lemma
54
.

Combining Lemma
52
and
53
,

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{p}_{\delta}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}{p_{\delta}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}=

\displaystyle\frac{p_{0}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{(\exp{(-\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{2k})}+\varepsilon_{1})}}{p_{0}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\exp{(-\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{1}{2k^{2}\delta}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon_{2})}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp{(\varepsilon_{2})}+\frac{\varepsilon_{1}}{\exp{(-\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{1}{2k^{2}\delta}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon_{2})}}

where
{\left|\varepsilon_{1}\right|}\leq 32{\mathcal{C}}_{k}(1+r^{4})
for
{\mathcal{C}}_{k}
as defined in (
E.11
) and
{\left|\varepsilon_{2}\right|}\leq\frac{2d}{k^{2}}
.

By Taylor Expansion,

\displaystyle{\left|\frac{\hat{p}_{\delta}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}{p_{\delta}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}}-1\right|}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle{(\frac{4d}{k^{2}}+\frac{32{\mathcal{C}}_{k}(1+r^{4})}{\exp{(-\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{1}{2k^{2}\delta}{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{u}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}-\frac{2d}{k^{2}})}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{12}{\mathcal{C}}_{k}^{2}{(1+r^{8})}+\frac{16d^{2}}{k^{4}}

where we use the fact that
\frac{d}{2k}+\frac{2d}{k^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4}
.

∎

E.2.1
Truncated Talagrand

Proof of Lemma
44
.

We first define
p^{\prime}
and
q^{\prime}
to be the density of
p
and
q
conditional over
B_{c}
, i.e.
p^{\prime}:=p/a
,
q^{\prime}:=q/b
. Think of
B_{r}
as a manifold equipped with the Euclidean distance (note this has nothing todo with the manifold
M
that we are considering). It is convex and connected. We verify that
p^{\prime}
satisfies the Bakry Emery condition with parameter
m
, and hence Talagrand’s inequality, with parameter
m
(see
Otto and Villani 2000
). Thus

\displaystyle W_{2}^{2}(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})\leq\frac{1}{m}KL(q^{\prime}||p^{\prime})

Furthermore, using the inequality
t\log(t)\leq t^{2}-t
(this step is almost exactly copied from
Zhai 2018

\displaystyle KL(q^{\prime}||p^{\prime})=

\displaystyle\int_{B_{c}}\frac{q^{\prime}(x)}{p^{\prime}(x)}\log{(\frac{q^{\prime}(x)}{p^{\prime}(x)})}dp^{\prime}(x)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q^{\prime}(x)}{p^{\prime}(x)})}^{2}-\frac{q^{\prime}(x)}{p^{\prime}(x)}dp^{\prime}(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q^{\prime}(x)}{p^{\prime}(x)}-1)}^{2}+\frac{q^{\prime}(x)}{p^{\prime}(x)}-1dp^{\prime}(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q^{\prime}(x)}{p^{\prime}(x)}-1)}^{2}dp^{\prime}(x)

By definition of
p^{\prime}
and
q^{\prime}
, we can further upper bound

\displaystyle\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q^{\prime}(x)}{p^{\prime}(x)}-1)}^{2}p^{\prime}(x)dx=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{a}\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{a}{b}\frac{q(x)}{p(x)}-1)}^{2}p(x)dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{a}{b^{2}}\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q(x)}{p(x)}-\frac{b}{a})}^{2}p(x)dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2a}{b^{2}}\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q(x)}{p(x)}-1)}^{2}p(x)dx+\frac{2a}{b^{2}}\int_{B_{c}}{(1-\frac{b}{a})}^{2}p(x)dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2a}{b^{2}}\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q(x)}{p(x)}-1)}^{2}p(x)dx+\frac{2}{ab^{2}}(a-b)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q(x)}{p(x)}-1)}^{2}p(x)dx+8(1-a)^{2}+8(1-b)^{2}

where the above use Young’s inequality, and our upper bound on
1-a
,
1-b
, and the fact that
a\leq 1
and
b\leq 1
.

Finally, to bound
W_{2}(p,q)
in terms of
W_{2}(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})
, we construct the following simple coupling: Let
x^{\prime},y^{\prime}
be drawn from the optimal coupling of
p^{\prime}
and
q^{\prime}
(i.e.
x\sim p^{\prime}
,
y\sim q^{\prime}
,
\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x-y\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]=W_{2}^{2}(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})
). Let
x^{c}\sim\left.p\right|_{B_{c}^{c}}
and
y^{c}\sim\left.q\right|_{B_{c}^{c}}
independently. Assume for now that
a\leq b
. We now let
x,y
be drawn from the following mixture process:

•

With probability
a
:
x:=x^{\prime}
,
y:=y^{\prime}

•

With probability
b-a
:
x:=x^{c}
,
y:=y^{\prime}

•

with probability
1-b
:
x:=x^{c}
,
y:=y^{c}

We first verify that the density of
x
is
a\cdot p^{\prime}+(b-a)\cdot p^{c}+(1-b)\cdot p^{c}=a\cdot p^{\prime}+(1-a)\cdot p^{c}=p
. We smilarly verify that the density of
y
is
q
. Therefore,
x,y
is a valid coupling between
p
and
q
. Under this coupling, the expected square distance is given by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x-y\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]=

\displaystyle a\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x^{\prime}-y^{\prime}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]+(b-a)\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x^{c}-y^{\prime}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]+(1-b)\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x^{c}-y^{c}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]

We bound each of the three terms above separately. First, from definition,
\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x^{\prime}-y^{\prime}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]=W_{2}^{2}(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})
. Next,

\displaystyle(b-a)\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x^{c}-y^{\prime}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle 2(b-a)\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x^{c}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]+2(b-a)\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|y^{\prime}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(b-a)\int{\mathbbm{1}\left\{{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}\geq r\right\}}{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}^{2}\frac{p(x)}{1-a}dx+2(b-a)\int{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{2}p(y)dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\int{\mathbbm{1}\left\{{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}\geq r\right\}}{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}^{2}p(x)dx+2(b-a)\int{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{2}p(y)dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(1-a)\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}^{4}}\right]}+2(b-a)\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]

where the first line is by Young’s inequality, the second line is by definition of
x^{c}
,
y^{c}
. The third line uses
b-a\leq 1-a
. The last line is by Cauchy Schwarz.

We now bound the third term:

\displaystyle(1-b)\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x^{c}-y^{c}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle 2(1-b)\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x^{c}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]+2(1-b)\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|y^{c}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2(1-b)\int{\mathbbm{1}\left\{{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}\geq r\right\}}{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}^{2}\frac{p(x)}{1-a}dx+2(1-b)\int{\mathbbm{1}\left\{{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}\geq r\right\}}{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{2}\frac{p(y)}{1-b}dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\int{\mathbbm{1}\left\{{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}\geq r\right\}}{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}^{2}p(x)dx+2\int{\mathbbm{1}\left\{{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}\geq r\right\}}{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{2}q(y)dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2(1-a)\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}^{4}}\right]}+2(1-b)\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{4}}\right]}

where the steps have very similar justifications as the preceding equation block. Note that we use
\frac{1-b}{1-a}\leq 1
as
b\geq a
. Putting everything together,

\displaystyle W_{2}^{2}(p,q)\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x-y\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle W_{2}^{2}(p^{\prime},q^{\prime})+4(1-a)\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}^{4}}\right]}+2(b-a)\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{2}}\right]+2(1-b)\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{4}}\right]}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{m}\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q(x)}{p(x)}-1)}^{2}p(x)dx+4(1-a)\cdot{(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}^{4}}\right]}+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{4}}\right]})}

Recall that we assumed that
a\leq b
. If instead
a>b
, then by similar steps, we have

\displaystyle W_{2}^{2}(p,q)\leq\frac{3}{m}\int_{B_{c}}{(\frac{q(x)}{p(x)}-1)}^{2}p(x)dx+4(1-b)\cdot{(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|x\right\|}_{2}^{4}}\right]}+\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left\|y\right\|}_{2}^{4}}\right]})}

This concludes our proof.

∎

E.2.2
Invertibility

Lemma 55
.

Let
{\mathbf{H}}
satisfy

\displaystyle\forall\mathbf{u}\qquad

\displaystyle{\left\|{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{\mathbf{H}}

\displaystyle\forall\mathbf{u}:{\left\|\mathbf{u}\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{L_{R}}}\qquad

\displaystyle{\left\|\nabla{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}

Let
{\mathcal{G}}(\mathbf{u}):=\mathbf{u}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})
.
Assume that
\delta\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{16L_{R}L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}},\frac{1}{16{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{2}}\right\}
.

Then for any
\mathbf{x}
satisfying
{\left\|\mathbf{x}\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{4\sqrt{L_{R}}}
,
{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}(\mathbf{x})
exists and is unique. Furthermore,
{\left\|{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}(\mathbf{x})\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{L_{R}}}

Proof of Lemma
55
.

Define the map
T(\mathbf{u}):=\mathbf{u}-\frac{1}{2}{(\mathbf{u}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})-\mathbf{x})}
. Let
\mathbf{u}_{0}
be arbitary, and let
\mathbf{u}_{k+1}=T(\mathbf{u}_{k})
. We first verify that for any initial
\mathbf{u}_{0}
, there exists a
i
such that for all
k\geq i
,
{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{L_{R}}}
. To see this,

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k+1}-\mathbf{x}\right\|}_{2}=

\displaystyle{\left\|T(\mathbf{u}_{k})-\mathbf{x}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\frac{1}{2}{(\mathbf{u}_{k}-x)}-\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{2}{{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u}_{k})}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}-\mathbf{x}\right\|}_{2}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{2}L_{\mathbf{H}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}-\mathbf{x}\right\|}_{2}+\frac{1}{8\sqrt{L_{R}}}

By induction, for all
k
,

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}-\mathbf{x}\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2^{k}}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{0}-\mathbf{x}\right\|}_{2}+\frac{1}{4\sqrt{L_{R}}}

our claim then follows by triangle inequality and our bound on
{\left\|\mathbf{x}\right\|}_{2}
.

We now verify that
T(\cdot)
is a contractive map. Consider any initial
\mathbf{u}_{0}
and
\mathbf{v}_{0}
. Let
k
be the minimum index such that
{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{j}\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{L_{R}}}
and
{\left\|\mathbf{v}_{j}\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{L_{R}}}
for all
j\geq k
. By our assumption, for all
s\in[0,1]
,
{\left\|\nabla{\mathbf{H}}(s\mathbf{u}_{k}+(1-s)\mathbf{v}_{k})\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}
. Integrating, we get
{\left\|{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u}_{j})-{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{v}_{j})\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{\mathbf{H}}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{j}-\mathbf{v}_{j}\right\|}_{2}
for all
j\geq k
. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k+1}-\mathbf{v}_{k+1}\right\|}_{2}=

\displaystyle{\left\|T(\mathbf{u}_{k})-T(\mathbf{v}_{k})\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|{(\mathbf{u}_{k}-\frac{1}{2}{(\mathbf{u}_{k}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u}_{k})-\mathbf{x})})}-{(\mathbf{v}_{k}-\frac{1}{2}{(\mathbf{v}_{k}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{v}_{k})-\mathbf{x})})}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}-\mathbf{v}_{k}\right\|}_{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\delta}{\left\|{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u}_{k})-{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{v}_{k})\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}-\mathbf{v}_{k}\right\|}_{2}+\frac{1}{4}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}-\mathbf{v}_{k}\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}-\mathbf{v}_{k}\right\|}_{2}

Using essentially the proof of Banach Fixed Point Theorem, there is a unique fixed point, which we denote
\mathbf{u}^{*}
. From our definitions, we verify that
{\mathcal{G}}^{-1}(\mathbf{x})=\mathbf{u}
if and only if
T(\mathbf{u})=\mathbf{u}
.
∎

E.2.3
Tail Bound

Consider the processes defined in (
E.2
). Both
\hat{\mathbf{z}}_{k}
and
\mathbf{z}_{k}
are sub-Gaussian:

Lemma 56
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\exp{(\frac{{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{z}}_{k}-k\delta{\bm{\beta}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{8k\delta L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}})}}\right]\leq 2

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\exp{(\frac{{\left\|\mathbf{z}_{k}-k\delta{\bm{\beta}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{8k\delta})}}\right]\leq\exp(d/8)

Consequently,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}{({{\left\|\hat{\mathbf{z}}_{k}-k\delta{\bm{\beta}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\geq t})}\leq 2\exp{(\frac{-t}{8k\delta L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}})}

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}{({{\left\|{\mathbf{z}}_{k}-k\delta{\bm{\beta}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\geq t})}\leq\exp{(\frac{-t+k\delta d}{8k\delta})}

Proof.

The bound for
\hat{\mathbf{z}}_{k}
follows from Lemma
57
, by taking
\mathbf{u}_{k}:=\mathbf{z}_{k}-k\delta\beta(\hat{y_{0}})
and
{\mathbf{H}}_{k}:={\mathbf{H}}_{k}(\hat{\mathbf{z}}_{k})
. We verify from Cauchy Schwarz that it suffices to take
L_{\mathbf{H}}:=L_{{\mathbf{H}}}
.

The bound for
\mathbf{z}_{k}
is because
\mathbf{z}_{k}-k\delta{\bm{\beta}}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,k\delta I)
. Thus
a:=\frac{1}{k\delta}{\left\|\mathbf{z}_{k}-k\delta{\bm{\beta}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}
is a Chi-square random variable with mean
d
, this it is sub-exponential with parameters
(2\sqrt{d},4)
. Thus
\mathbb{E}\left[{e^{(a-d)/8}}\right]\leq e^{d/32}
. Plugging in the definition for
a
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\exp{(\frac{{\left\|\mathbf{z}_{k}-k\delta{\bm{\beta}}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{8K\delta})}}\right]\leq\exp(d/8)

The probability bounds are by Markov’s Inequality.

∎

Lemma 57
.

Let
\mathbf{u}_{k+1}=\mathbf{u}_{k}+\sqrt{\delta}{\mathbf{H}}_{k}
be a martingale. Assume that for all
c\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
\mathbb{E}\left[{\exp{(\bm{\left\langle}c,{\mathbf{H}}_{k}\bm{})}}\right]\leq{\left\|c\right\|}_{2}\cdot L_{\mathbf{H}}
a.s. Then for all
k
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\exp{(\frac{{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{8k\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}})}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle 2

Proof.

Consider an arbitrary
i<k
. Then

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{i+1}\right\|}_{2}^{2}=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\mathbf{u}_{i},{\mathbf{H}}_{i}\bm{}+\delta{\left\|{\mathbf{H}}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{k}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\mathbf{u}_{i},{\mathbf{H}}_{i}\bm{}+\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}

Taking expectation,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{{\mathcal{F}}_{i}}}\left[{\exp{(s{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{i+1}\right\|}_{2}^{2})}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp{(s{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+s\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2})}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{{{\mathcal{F}}_{i}}}\left[{\exp{(-s\sqrt{\delta}\bm{\left\langle}\mathbf{u}_{i},{\mathbf{H}}_{i}\bm{})}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp{(s{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+s\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2})}\cdot{\exp{(s^{2}\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2})}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\exp{(s{(1+s\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2})}{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}+s\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2})}

Applying the above recursively, and choosing
s:=\frac{1}{8k\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\exp{(\frac{{\left\|\mathbf{u}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}}{8k\delta L_{\mathbf{H}}^{2}})}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle 2

∎

E.2.4
Other Algebra

Lemma 58
.

Let
{\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})
satisfy, for all
\mathbf{u}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{(\mathbf{u})}}\right]=0\qquad\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{(\mathbf{u})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\mathbf{u})}^{T}}\right]=I

Then for any
\hat{\mathbf{u}}
such that
\nabla{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})
exists,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+H{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}}\right]=0

and for any
\hat{\mathbf{u}}
such that
\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}(\hat{\mathbf{u}})
exists,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{2\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}+\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})}+\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2}}\right]=0

Proof of Lemma
58
.

Let us define
D(\mathbf{u}):=\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{(\mathbf{u})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\mathbf{u})}^{T}}\right]
. Clearly
D(\mathbf{u})=I
for all
\mathbf{u}
. Thus

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\partial_{j}D_{i,j}(\mathbf{u})=0

For simplicity, we omit the explicit dependence on
\mathbf{u}
below, i.e.
{\mathbf{H}}:={\mathbf{H}}(\mathbf{u})
and
D:=D(\mathbf{u})

With some algebra, we verify that

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\partial_{j}D_{i,j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\partial_{j}\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{\mathbf{H}}^{T}}\right]_{i,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\partial_{j}\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}^{i}{\mathbf{H}}^{j}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\mathbb{E}\left[{{(\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{i})}{\mathbf{H}}^{j}+{(\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{j})}{\mathbf{H}}^{i}}\right]

We verify from algebra that

\displaystyle{\left[\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+H{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\right]}_{i}=\sum_{j}\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{i}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}^{j}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+\sum_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{i}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{j}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}

Thus we get our first conclusion: for all
i
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{{\left[\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}+H{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}\right]}_{i}}\right]=\sum_{j}\partial_{j}D_{i,j}=0

Taking another derivative of
\sum_{j}\partial_{j}D_{i,j}(\mathbf{u})=0
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\partial_{i}\partial_{j}D_{i,j}=0

With some algebra, we verify that

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\partial_{i}\partial_{j}D_{i,j}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\partial_{i}\partial_{j}\mathbb{E}\left[{{\mathbf{H}}{\mathbf{H}}^{T}}\right]_{i,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\mathbb{E}\left[{{(\partial_{i}\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{i})}{\mathbf{H}}^{j}+{(\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{i})}{(\partial_{i}{\mathbf{H}}^{j})}+{(\partial_{i}\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{j})}{\mathbf{H}}^{i}+{(\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{j})}{(\partial_{i}{\mathbf{H}}^{i})}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\mathbb{E}\left[{2{(\partial_{i}\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{i})}{\mathbf{H}}^{j}+{(\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{i})}{(\partial_{i}{\mathbf{H}}^{j})}+{(\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{j})}{(\partial_{i}{\mathbf{H}}^{i})}}\right]

We also verify that

\displaystyle\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,i}\partial_{j}\partial_{i}{\mathbf{H}}^{i}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\cdot H^{j}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}

\displaystyle\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{i}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\cdot\partial_{i}{\mathbf{H}}^{j}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}

\displaystyle\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\partial_{i}{\mathbf{H}}^{i}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\cdot\partial_{j}{\mathbf{H}}^{j}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}

Thus combining the above,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{2\text{tr}{(\bm{\left\langle}\nabla^{2}{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})},{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})}\bm{})}+\text{tr}{({(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2})}+\text{tr}{(\nabla{\mathbf{H}}{(\hat{\mathbf{u}})})}^{2}}\right]=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j}\partial_{i}\partial_{j}D_{i,j}=0

This proves our second claim.

∎

Lemma 59
.

Let
A\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times d}
, and let
{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}<\frac{1}{4}
. Then

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(I+A)}-{(\text{tr}{(A)}-\frac{\text{tr}{(A^{2})}}{2})}\right|}\leq 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{3}

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(I+A)}-\text{tr}{(A)}\right|}\leq 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle{\left|\log\det{(I+A)}\right|}\leq 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}

Proof.

By assumption on
A
,
I+A
in invertible. Thus using (a corollary of) the Jacobi Formula,

\displaystyle\log\det{(e^{I+A})}=

\displaystyle\text{tr}\log{(I+A)}

By series expansion of matrix
\log
,

\displaystyle\log{(I+A)}=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}{(-1)}^{i+1}\frac{{(A)}^{i}}{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A-\frac{A^{2}}{2}+\sum_{i=3}^{d}{(-1)}^{i+1}\frac{{(A)}^{i}}{i}

The last term can be bounded as

\displaystyle{\left|\text{tr}{(\sum_{i=3}^{d}{(-1)}^{i+1}\frac{{(A)}^{i}}{i})}\right|}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=3}^{d}\frac{1}{i}{\left|\text{tr}{(A^{i})}\right|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=3}^{d}\frac{1}{i}{(\sqrt{\text{tr}{({(AA^{T})}^{i})}}\cdot\sqrt{d})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=3}^{d}\frac{1}{i}d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{i}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{3}\sum_{i=3}^{d}{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{i-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{3}\sum_{i=3}^{d}{(\frac{1}{4})}^{i-3}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{3}

Using similar arguments, but truncating at
i=2
instead of
i=3
,

\displaystyle\log{(I+A)}=A+\sum_{i=2}^{d}{(-1)}^{i+1}\frac{{(A)}^{i}}{i}

and the last term can be bounded as

\displaystyle{\left|\sum_{i=2}^{d}{(-1)}^{i+1}\frac{{(A)}^{i}}{i}\right|}\leq 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}^{2}

we skip the steps as they are identical to the preceding proof.

Finally, the last desired inequality involves bounding the entire sum

\displaystyle{\left|\sum_{i=3}^{d}{(-1)}^{i+1}\frac{{(A)}^{i}}{i}\right|}\leq 2d{\left\|A\right\|}_{2}

∎

Lemma 60
.

Let
{\mathcal{C}}_{k}
be as defined in (
E.11
). Then for any
K,r
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{k\delta}{({\mathcal{C}}_{k}+\frac{d}{k^{2}})}={(\delta^{3/2}K\lambda_{1}+\delta\sqrt{K}\lambda_{2}+\sqrt{\delta}\log(K)\lambda_{3}+\sqrt{\delta}\lambda_{4}+\delta^{2}K\lambda_{5})}

for
\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2},\lambda_{3},\lambda_{4},\lambda_{5}=poly{(L_{\mathbf{H}},L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime},L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime\prime})}
defined in (
E.12
).

Proof.

Recall from (
E.11
) that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{k}:=

\displaystyle\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{k}}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{1}{k^{3/2}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{4}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{3/2}d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+\delta^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{3/2}d^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+\delta^{2}{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{4})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}dL_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\frac{\delta}{k}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{k}}d{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}+\frac{\delta^{3/2}}{\sqrt{k}}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{3}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{d^{2}}{k^{2}}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{3}}{k^{3/2}}+\frac{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{6}}{k^{3}}+\delta^{3/2}d^{3}{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{3}

Let us reorganize the terms above, and group them by their dependence on
\delta
and
k
:

\displaystyle 1.\ \frac{\delta}{\sqrt{k}}{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}+d{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{3}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})})}

\displaystyle 2.\ \frac{\sqrt{\delta}}{k}{((2+d)L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3})})}

\displaystyle 3.\ \frac{1}{k^{3/2}}{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3})}+\frac{1}{k^{2}}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{4}+d^{2}+1)}+\frac{1}{k^{3}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{6}

\displaystyle 4.\ \delta^{3/2}{(d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}+d^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+d^{3}{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{3}+\sqrt{\delta}{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{4})})}

(
E.11
)

Then

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{k\delta}{({\mathcal{C}}_{k}+\frac{d}{k^{2}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}K\cdot{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}+d{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{2}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2})}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})^{3}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})})}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta\sqrt{K}\cdot{((2+d)L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+\sqrt{\delta}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3})})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\delta}\log(K)\cdot{(2L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{3})}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\delta}\cdot{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{4}+d^{2}+1+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{6})}

\displaystyle\quad+\delta^{2}K\cdot{(d{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{2}+L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}+d^{2}{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{3})}+d^{3}{L_{\mathbf{H}}^{\prime}}^{3}+\sqrt{\delta}{({(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime\prime}L_{{\mathbf{H}}})}^{2}+{(L_{{\mathbf{H}}}^{\prime})}^{4})})}

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\delta^{3/2}K\lambda_{1}+\delta\sqrt{K}\lambda_{2}+\sqrt{\delta}\log(K)\lambda_{3}+\sqrt{\delta}\lambda_{4}+\delta^{2}K\lambda_{5}

(E.12)

∎

Appendix F
Fundamental Manifold Results

In this section, we provide Taylor expansion style inequalities for the evolution of geodesics on manifold. By making use of tools from Matrix ODE, we can bound the distance between two points along geodesics under various conditions. To do so, we start by bounding the variation of Jacobi field by Riemannian curvature.

F.1
Jacobi Field Approximations

Lemma 61
.

Let
\Lambda(s,t):[0,1]\times[0,1]\to M
be a field of variations, where for each fixed
s
,
t\to\Lambda(s,t)
is a geodesic. Let us define
{\mathcal{C}}:=\sqrt{L_{R}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{2}}
. Then for all
s,t\in[0,1]
,

\displaystyle{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}{(\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)+tD_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0))}\right\|}\leq{(\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}-1)}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+{(\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}-1)}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}{(\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0))}\right\|}\leq{(\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}-1)}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}\leq{{\mathcal{C}}}\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}{(D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0))}\right\|}\leq{{\mathcal{C}}}\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+{(\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}-1)}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

If in addition, the derivative of the Riemannian curvature tensor is globally bounded by
L_{R}^{\prime}
, then

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}{(D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0))}-t\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}{(D_{t}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0))}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}^{4})}e^{{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}^{2})}e^{{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

Proof.

For any fixed
s
, let
E_{i}(s,0)
be a basis of
T_{\Lambda(s,0)}M
.

Let
E_{i}(s,t)
denote an orthonormal frame along
\gamma_{s}(t):=\Lambda(s,t)
, by parallel transporting
E_{i}(s,0)
.

Let
\mathbf{J}(s,t)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
denote the coordinates of
\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)
wrt
E_{i}(s,t)
. Let
\mathbf{K}(s,t)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
denote the coordinates of
D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)
wrt
E_{i}(s,t)
. Let
\mathbf{a}(s,t)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
denote the coordinates of
\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,t)
wrt
E_{i}(s,t)
(this is constant for fixed
s
, for all
t
). Let
\mathbf{R}(s,t)\in\mathbb{R}^{4d}
be such that
\mathbf{R}^{i}_{j,k}=\bm{\left\langle}R(E_{j}(s,t),E_{k}(s,t))E_{l}(s,t),E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}
. Let
\mathbf{M}(s,t)
denote the matrix with

\mathbf{M}_{i,j}(s,t):=-\sum_{k,l}\mathbf{R}^{i}_{jkl}(s,t)\mathbf{a}_{k}(s,t)\mathbf{a}_{l}(s,t)
. We verify that
{\left\|\mathbf{M}(s,t)\right\|}_{2}\leq L_{R}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}^{2}=L_{R}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{2}
.

The Jacobi Equation states that
D_{t}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)=-R(\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t),\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,t))\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,t)
. We verify that
-\bm{\left\langle}R(\partial_{s},\partial_{t})\partial_{t},E_{i}\bm{}=-\sum_{j,k,l}\mathbf{R}^{i}_{jkl}\mathbf{J}_{j}\mathbf{a}_{k}\mathbf{a}_{l}={\left[\mathbf{M}(s,t)\mathbf{J}(s,t)\right]}_{i}
, thus
D_{t}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)=\sum_{i=1}^{d}{\left[\mathbf{M}(s,t)\mathbf{J}(s,t)\right]}_{i}\cdot E_{i}(s,t)
.

We verify that
\frac{d}{dt}\mathbf{J}_{i}(s,t)=D_{t}\bm{\left\langle}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t),E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}=\bm{\left\langle}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t),E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}=\mathbf{K}_{i}(s,t)
. We also verify that
\frac{d}{dt}\mathbf{K}_{i}(s,t)=D_{t}\bm{\left\langle}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t),E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}=\bm{\left\langle}D_{t}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t),E_{i}(s,t)\bm{}={\left[\mathbf{M}(s,t)\mathbf{J}(s,t)\right]}_{i}
.

Let us now consider a fixed
s
. To simplify notation, we drop the
s
dependence. The Jacobi Equation, in coordinate form, corresponds to the following second-order ODE:

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathbf{J}(t)=\mathbf{K}(t)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathbf{K}(t)=\mathbf{M}(t)\mathbf{J}(t)dt

Define
L_{\mathbf{M}}:=L_{R}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{2}={\mathcal{C}}^{2}
. We verify that
L_{\mathbf{M}}\geq\max_{t\in[0,1]}{\left\|\mathbf{M}(t)\right\|}_{2}
. Then from
79
, we see that

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{J}(t)\\
\mathbf{K}(t)\end{bmatrix}=\emat{(t;\mathbf{M})}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{J}(0)\\
\mathbf{K}(0)\end{bmatrix}

From Lemma
80
,

\displaystyle\emat{(t;\begin{bmatrix}0&I\\
\mathbf{M}(t)&0\end{bmatrix})}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{A}(t)&\mathbf{B}(t)\\
\mathbf{C}(t)&\mathbf{D}(t)\end{bmatrix}

where each block is
\mathbb{R}^{2d}
, and can be bounded as

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{A}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}}t)}\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{B}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}}t)}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}\leq\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{C}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq{{\mathcal{C}}}\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}}t)}\leq{{\mathcal{C}}}\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{D}(t)\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}}t)}\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{A}(t)-I\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}}t)}-1\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}-1

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{B}(t)-tI\right\|}_{2}\leq\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}}t)}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}-t\leq\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}-1

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{D}(t)-I\right\|}_{2}\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}}t)}-1\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}-1

where we use the fact that
\cosh(r)
,
\sinh(r)
and
\frac{\sinh(r)}{r}
are monotonically increasing and that
\frac{\sinh(r)}{r}-1\geq 0
for positive
r
.

It follows that

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}(t)=

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}(t)\mathbf{J}(0)+\mathbf{B}(t)\mathbf{K}(0)

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}(t)=

\displaystyle\mathbf{C}(t)\mathbf{J}(0)+\mathbf{D}(t)\mathbf{K}(0)

(F.1)

Thus

\displaystyle{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{J}(t)\right\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{A}(t)\mathbf{J}(0)+\mathbf{B}(t)\mathbf{K}(0)\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

and

\displaystyle{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\left\{\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)+tD_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\}^{\to\Lambda(s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{J}(t)-\mathbf{J}(0)-t\mathbf{K}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|{(\mathbf{A}(t)-I)}\mathbf{J}(0)+{(\mathbf{B}(t)-tI)}\mathbf{K}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}-1)}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+{(\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}-1)}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

and

\displaystyle{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\left\{\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\}^{\to\Lambda(s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|{(\mathbf{A}(t)-I)}\mathbf{J}(0)+\mathbf{B}(t)\mathbf{K}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}-1)}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

Similarly,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)\right\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{K}(t)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{C}(t)\mathbf{J}(0)\right\|}_{2}+{\left\|\mathbf{D}(t)\mathbf{K}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{C}}}\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

and

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\left\{D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\}^{\to\Lambda(s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{K}(t)-\mathbf{K}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{C}(t)\mathbf{J}(0)+{(\mathbf{D}(t)-I)}\mathbf{K}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{{\mathcal{C}}}\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+{(\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}-1)}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

To prove the last bound, let us define
L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}:=L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{3}
. We verify that

L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}\geq\max_{t\in[0,1]}{\left\|\mathbf{M}(t)-\mathbf{M}(0)\right\|}_{2}
.

we know that

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\left\{D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\}^{\to\Lambda(s,t)}-t\left\{D_{t}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\}^{\to\Lambda(s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\mathbf{K}(t)-\mathbf{K}(0)-t\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{J}(0)\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\mathbf{M}(r)\mathbf{J}(r)-\mathbf{M}(0)\mathbf{J}(0)dr\right\|}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}{\left\|\mathbf{M}(r)-\mathbf{M}(0)\right\|}_{2}{\left\|\mathbf{J}(0)\right\|}_{2}dr+\int_{0}^{t}{\left\|\mathbf{M}(r)\right\|}_{2}{\left\|\mathbf{J}(r)-\mathbf{J}(0)\right\|}_{2}dr

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}L_{\mathbf{M}}^{\prime}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,r)\right\|}+L_{\mathbf{M}}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,r)-\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,r)}{(\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0))}\right\|}dr

where the last line follows from (
F.1
). From our earlier results in this lemma,

\displaystyle{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,r)\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq e^{{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+e^{{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

\displaystyle{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\Gamma_{\Lambda(s,0)}^{\Lambda(s,t)}{(\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0))}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq{(\cosh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}-1)}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+\frac{\sinh{({{\mathcal{C}}})}}{{{\mathcal{C}}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq{{\mathcal{C}}}^{2}e^{{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+e^{{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

where the simplifications are from Lemma
14
. Put together,

\displaystyle{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,t)-\left\{D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\}^{\to\Lambda(s,t)}-t\left\{D_{t}D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\}^{\to\Lambda(s,t)}\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}^{4})}e^{{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}+{(L_{R}^{\prime}{\left\|\partial_{t}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}^{3}+{\mathcal{C}}^{2})}e^{{\mathcal{C}}}{\left\|D_{t}\partial_{s}\Lambda(s,0)\right\|}

∎

F.2
Discrete Coupling

Lemma 62
.

Let
x,y,z\in M
, with
x=\Exp_{z}(u)
,
y=\Exp_{z}(v)
.

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}(x,y)\leq\frac{\sinh{(\sqrt{L_{R}}{({\left\|u\right\|}+{\left\|v\right\|})}t)}}{\sqrt{L_{R}}{({\left\|u\right\|}+{\left\|v\right\|})}}{\left\|v-u\right\|}

Proof.
